File: ./App.tsx
----------------------------------------
import { enableScreens } from 'react-native-screens';
enableScreens();
import React from 'react';
import { createDrawerNavigator } from '@react-navigation/drawer';
import { NavigationContainer } from '@react-navigation/native';
import HomeScreen from './components/HomeScreen';
import CalculationPage from './components/calcualtionPage/CalculationPage';
import SignInScreen from './signIN/signInScreen';


const Drawer = createDrawerNavigator();

const App = () => (
  <NavigationContainer>
  <Drawer.Navigator initialRouteName="SignIn">
    <Drawer.Screen name="Log Out" component={SignInScreen} options={{ headerShown: false }}/>
    <Drawer.Screen name="Home" component={HomeScreen} />
    <Drawer.Screen name="CalculationPage" component={CalculationPage} />
  </Drawer.Navigator>
</NavigationContainer>
);

export default App;

File: ./components/main-table/MainTable.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React from "react";
import {
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  FlatList,
  Button,
  Platform,
} from "react-native";
import Duty from "../duty/Duty";
import { leaveTypes, monthNames } from "../../utils/constants";
import DutyRow from "../duty/DutyRow";
import EndDutyModal from "../end-duty-modal/EndDutyModal";
import Modal from "../modal/Modal";
import { NavigationProp } from "@react-navigation/native";
import SummaryBlock from "../summary-block/SummaryBlock";
import calculateLeaveMileage from "../allowancecalculation/leaveMileage";
import calculateDearnessAllowance from "../allowancecalculation/dearnessAllowance";
import calculateTransportationAllowance from "../allowancecalculation/transportationAllowance";
import calculateOsdaAllowance from "../allowancecalculation/osdaAllowance";
import computeNightDutyAllowance from "../allowancecalculation/nightDutyAllowance";
import mileageCalculation from "../allowancecalculation/mileageCalculation";
import PdfGeneratorMobile from "../pdfGeneration/PdfGenerator.mobile";
import Trip from "../trip/Trip";
import FortnightBlock from "../fortnight-block/FortnightBlock";
import Fortnight from "../fortnight-block/Fortnight";

let PDFGenerator: JSX.IntrinsicAttributes | null = null;
if (Platform.OS === "web") {
  PDFGenerator = require("../pdfGeneration/PDFGenerator").default;
}

interface MainTableProps {
  navigation: NavigationProp<any>;
  selectedMonth: string;
  rateOfPay: Number;
  designation: string;
}

interface MainTableState {
  duties: Duty[];
  showModal: boolean;
  selectedDuty: Duty | null;
  totalLeaves: number;
  overtimeResult: OverTimeCalculationResult | null;
  progressiveDutyHours: number;
  fortnights: Fortnight[];
}

class MainTable extends React.Component<MainTableProps, MainTableState> {
  state: MainTableState = {
    duties: [],
    showModal: false,
    selectedDuty: null,
    totalLeaves: 0,
    overtimeResult: 0,
    progressiveDutyHours: 0,
    fortnights: [],
  };

  constructor(props: MainTableProps) {
    super(props);
    const monthNumber = monthNames[this.props.selectedMonth];
    const paddedMonth = String(monthNumber).padStart(2, "0");

    const trip: Trip = {
      id: 1,
      date: `${new Date().getFullYear()}-${paddedMonth}-01`,
      typeOfWork: "",
      arrivalTime: "",
      departureTime: "",
      timeDifference: 0,
      sourceStation: "",
      destinationStation: "",
      trainNumber: "",
      distance: 0,
      allowance: 1,
      remarks: "",
      dutyHours: "",
    };

    const duty: Duty = new Duty(1, [trip]);

    this.state = {
      duties: [duty],
      showModal: false,
      selectedDuty: null,
      totalLeaves: 0,
    };
  }

  addDuty = () => {
    const monthNumber = monthNames[this.props.selectedMonth];
    const paddedMonth = String(monthNumber).padStart(2, "0");
    const newTrip: Trip = {
      id: 1,
      date: `${new Date().getFullYear()}-${paddedMonth}-01`,
      typeOfWork: "",
      arrivalTime: "",
      departureTime: "",
      timeDifference: 0,
      sourceStation: "",
      destinationStation: "",
      distance: 0,
      allowance: 0,
      remarks: "",
      trainNumber: "",
      dutyHours: "",
    };

    const newDuty = new Duty(this.state.duties.length + 1, [newTrip]);
    this.setState((prevState) => ({
      duties: [...prevState.duties, newDuty],
    }));
  };

  addTrip = (dutyId: number) => {
    this.setState((prevState) => ({
      duties: prevState.duties.map((duty) => {
        if (duty.id === dutyId) {
          const monthNumber = monthNames[this.props.selectedMonth];
          const paddedMonth = String(monthNumber).padStart(2, "0");
          const newTrip: Trip = {
            id: duty.trips.length + 1,
            date: `${new Date().getFullYear()}-${paddedMonth}-01`,
            typeOfWork: "",
            arrivalTime: "",
            departureTime: "",
            timeDifference: 0,
            sourceStation: "",
            destinationStation: "",
            distance: 0,
            allowance: 0,
            remarks: "",
            trainNumber: "",
            dutyHours: "",
          };
          return new Duty(duty.id, [...duty.trips, newTrip]);
        }
        return duty;
      }),
    }));
  };

  handleEndDuty = (duty: Duty) => {
    this.setState((prevState) => {
      const updatedDuties = prevState.duties.map((d) => {
        if (d.id === duty.id) {
          return duty;
        }
        return d;
      });

      // Filter non-leave duties
      const nonLeaveDuties = updatedDuties.filter((d) => !d.isLeave);

      // Sort duties by signIn time
      const sortedDuties = nonLeaveDuties.sort((a, b) => {
        return new Date(a.signIn).getTime() - new Date(b.signIn).getTime();
      });

      // Calculate cumulative night duty hours and update progressive night duty
      let cumulativeNightDutyHours = 0;
      sortedDuties.forEach((d) => {
        cumulativeNightDutyHours += d.nightDutyHours;
        d.progressiveNightDutyHours = cumulativeNightDutyHours;
      });

      return {
        duties: updatedDuties,
        selectedDuty: duty,
        showModal: true,
      };
    });
  };

  updateProgressiveNightDutyHours = () => {
    this.setState((prevState) => {
      const duties = [...prevState.duties];

      // Filter duties with signIn and signOff
      const completedDuties = duties.filter(
        (duty) => duty.signIn && duty.signOff
      );

      // Sort completed duties by signIn time
      const sortedDuties = completedDuties.sort((a, b) => {
        return new Date(a.signIn).getTime() - new Date(b.signIn).getTime();
      });

      // Calculate cumulative night duty hours and update progressive night duty
      let cumulativeNightDutyHours = 0;
      sortedDuties.forEach((duty) => {
        if (!duty.isLeave) {
          cumulativeNightDutyHours += duty.nightDutyHours;
          duty.progressiveNightDutyHours = cumulativeNightDutyHours;
        }
      });

      return {
        duties: duties,
      };
    });
  };

  updateDutyDetails = (updatedDuty: Duty) => {
    this.setState(
      (prevState) => ({
        duties: prevState.duties.map((duty) => {
          if (duty.id === updatedDuty.id) {
            return updatedDuty;
          }
          return duty;
        }),
        showModal: false,
      }),
      () => {
        // Call updateProgressiveNightDutyHours after the state is updated
        if (updatedDuty.signIn && updatedDuty.signOff) {
          this.updateProgressiveNightDutyHours();
        }
      }
    );
  };

  updateTripData = (
    dutyId: number,
    tripId: number,
    field: keyof Trip,
    value: string | number
  ) => {
    this.setState(
      (prevState) => ({
        duties: prevState.duties.map((duty) => {
          if (duty.id === dutyId) {
            const tripToUpdate = duty.trips.find((trip) => trip.id === tripId);
            if (tripToUpdate) {
              const updatedTrip = { ...tripToUpdate, [field]: value };
              if (field === "typeOfWork") {
                const isLeave = leaveTypes.includes(value as string);
                const wasLeave = leaveTypes.includes(tripToUpdate.typeOfWork);
                if (isLeave && !wasLeave) {
                  this.setState((prevState) => ({
                    totalLeaves: prevState.totalLeaves + 1,
                  }));
                  // Reset arrival and departure time, sign-on and sign-off times, and arrival and departure stations
                  updatedTrip.arrivalTime = "";
                  updatedTrip.departureTime = "";
                  updatedTrip.sourceStation = "";
                  updatedTrip.destinationStation = "";
                  duty.signIn = null;
                  duty.signOff = null;
                  duty.isLeave = true;
                } else if (!isLeave && wasLeave) {
                  this.setState((prevState) => ({
                    totalLeaves: Math.max(prevState.totalLeaves - 1, 0),
                  }));
                }
              }

              duty.updateTrip(tripId, updatedTrip);
            }
          }
          return duty;
        }),
      }),
      () => {
        // Call handleDutiesChange after state is updated
        this.handleDutiesChange(this.state.duties);
      }
    );
  };

  calculateTotalDutyAllowance = () => {
    const { duties } = this.state;
    const totalAllowance = duties.reduce(
      (acc, duty) => acc + duty.dutyAllowance,
      0
    );
    return totalAllowance;
  };

  isPreviousDutyEnded = () => {
    const { duties } = this.state;
    const lastDuty = duties[duties.length - 1];
    if (!lastDuty) {
      return false;
    }
    return lastDuty.hasEnded;
  };

  deleteTrip = (dutyId: number, tripId: number) => {
    this.setState((prevState) => ({
      duties: prevState.duties.map((duty) => {
        if (duty.id === dutyId) {
          const updatedTrips = duty.trips.filter((trip) => trip.id !== tripId);
          return new Duty(duty.id, updatedTrips);
        }
        return duty;
      }),
    }));
  };

  calculateTotalNightDutyHours = (): number => {
    const { duties } = this.state;
    const totalNightDutyHours = duties.reduce(
      (acc, duty) => acc + duty.nightDutyHours,
      0
    );

    return totalNightDutyHours;
  };

  deleteDuty = (dutyId: number) => {
    this.setState((prevState) => {
      const updatedDuties = prevState.duties.filter(
        (duty) => duty.id !== dutyId
      );

      // If all the duties are deleted, there should be a default duty present.

      if (updatedDuties.length === 0) {
        const monthNumber = monthNames[this.props.selectedMonth];
        const paddedMonth = String(monthNumber).padStart(2, "0");

        const newTrip: Trip = {
          id: 1,
          date: `${new Date().getFullYear()}-${paddedMonth}-01`,
          typeOfWork: "",
          arrivalTime: "",
          departureTime: "",
          timeDifference: 0,
          sourceStation: "",
          destinationStation: "",
          distance: 0,
          allowance: 0,
          remarks: "",
          trainNumber: "",
          dutyHours: "",
        };

        const newDuty = new Duty(1, [newTrip]);
        return { duties: [newDuty] };
      }

      return { duties: updatedDuties };
    });
  };

  setTotalLeaves = (newTotalLeaves: number) => {
    this.setState({ totalLeaves: newTotalLeaves });
  };

  handleDutiesChange = (updatedDuties: Duty[] | any[]) => {
    let startDate = null;
    if (updatedDuties.length > 0 && updatedDuties[0].trips.length > 0) {
      const firstTripDate = updatedDuties[0].trips[0].date;
      if (firstTripDate) {
        startDate = new Date(firstTripDate);
      }
    }
  };

  handleOvertimeAllowanceChange = (newAllowance: number) => {
    this.setState({ overtimeResult: newAllowance });
  };

  handleFortnightsChange = (fortnights: Fortnight[]) => {
    this.setState({ fortnights });
  };

  render() {
    const { overtimeResult, fortnights } = this.state;

    const { duties, showModal, selectedDuty, totalLeaves } = this.state;
    const { rateOfPay, selectedMonth, designation, payBand, level } =
      this.props;

    let startDate = ""; // Default to an empty string

    if (duties.length > 0 && duties[0].trips.length > 0) {
      startDate = duties[0].trips[0].date;
    }

    // Calculate the number of days in the month from the first trip date if available// Assuming selectedMonth is passed as a prop and is the full name of the month
    const year = new Date().getFullYear(); // Use the current year or another relevant year

    let daysInMonth = 1;
    if (selectedMonth && monthNames[selectedMonth]) {
      const month = monthNames[selectedMonth];
      daysInMonth = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate(); // Get the total number of days in the selected month
    }

    // Calculate leave mileage allowance
    const { leaveMileageAllowance } = calculateLeaveMileage({
      rateOfPay,
      daysInMonth,
      duties,
    });

    // Calculate total kilometers from duties
    let totalKilometers = duties.reduce((acc, duty) => {
      const dutyDistance = duty.trips.reduce(
        (tripAcc, trip) =>
          tripAcc + (typeof trip.distance === "number" ? trip.distance : 0),
        0
      );
      return acc + dutyDistance;
    }, 0);

    const totalDutyAllowance = this.calculateTotalDutyAllowance();
    const mileage = mileageCalculation(designation, totalDutyAllowance); // Example mileage rate, replace as needed

    const nationalHolidayAllowance = 1; // Example fixed national holiday allowance, replace as needed
    const overTimeAllowance = 75; // Example overtitotalAllowanceme allowance, replace as needed
    const dearnessAllowance = calculateDearnessAllowance(rateOfPay);
    const transportationAllowance =
      calculateTransportationAllowance(designation);
    const totalNightDutyHours = this.calculateTotalNightDutyHours();
    const nightDutyAllowance = computeNightDutyAllowance(
      rateOfPay,
      dearnessAllowance,
      totalNightDutyHours
    );
    const osdaCount = this.state.duties.reduce(
      (count, duty) => (duty.isOSDA ? count + 1 : count),
      0
    );
    const rraCount = this.state.duties.reduce(
      (count, duty) => (duty.isRRA ? count + 1 : count),
      0
    );

    const osdaAllowance = calculateOsdaAllowance(designation, osdaCount);

    const totalAllowance =
      mileage +
      nightDutyAllowance +
      osdaAllowance +
      leaveMileageAllowance +
      overtimeResult; // OT to be added

    const lastDuty = duties[duties.length - 1];
    // const progressiveDutyHours = lastDuty ? lastDuty.progressiveDutyHours : 0;
    const grandTotalAllowance =
      (nightDutyAllowance || 0) +
      (leaveMileageAllowance || 0) +
      (osdaAllowance || 0);

    const calculateTotalDutyHours = (duties: Duty[]): number => {
      return duties.reduce((total, duty) => {
        return (
          total +
          duty.trips.reduce((dutyTotal, trip) => {
            const dutyHours = parseFloat(trip.dutyHours);
            return dutyTotal + (isNaN(dutyHours) ? 0 : dutyHours);
          }, 0)
        );
      }, 0);
    };

    const totalDutyHours = calculateTotalDutyHours(duties);

    return (
      <ScrollView
        horizontal
        style={styles.container}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.horizontalContent}
      >
        <View style={styles.tableContainer}>
          <FlatList
            data={duties}
            renderItem={({ item: duty }) => (
              <DutyRow
                key={duty.id}
                duty={duty}
                addTrip={this.addTrip}
                handleEndDuty={this.handleEndDuty}
                updateTripData={this.updateTripData}
                selectedMonth={selectedMonth}
                deleteTrip={this.deleteTrip}
                setTotalLeaves={this.setTotalLeaves}
                deleteDuty={this.deleteDuty}
              />
            )}
          />
        </View>
        <View style={styles.buttonContainer}>
          <View style={styles.addDuty}>
            <Button
              title="Add Duty"
              onPress={this.addDuty}
              disabled={!this.isPreviousDutyEnded()}
              color={colors.primary}
            />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.addDuty}>
            {Platform.OS === "web" && (
              <PDFGenerator
                duties={duties}
                designation={designation}
                payBand={payBand}
                level={level}
                rateOfPay={rateOfPay}
                selectedMonth={selectedMonth}
                totalAllowance={totalDutyAllowance}
                mileage={mileage}
                nightDutyAllowance={nightDutyAllowance}
                leaveMileageAllowance={leaveMileageAllowance}
                dearnessAllowance={dearnessAllowance}
                progressiveDutyHours={totalDutyHours}
                rraOsda={osdaAllowance}
                overtimeResult={overtimeResult}
                grandTotalAllowance={totalAllowance}
                fortnights={fortnights}
              />
            )}
            {Platform.OS !== "web" && (
              <PdfGeneratorMobile
                duties={duties}
                designation={designation}
                payBand={payBand}
                level={level}
                rateOfPay={rateOfPay}
                selectedMonth={selectedMonth}
                mileage={mileage}
                nightDutyAllowance={nightDutyAllowance}
                leaveMileageAllowance={leaveMileageAllowance}
                dearnessAllowance={dearnessAllowance}
                progressiveDutyHours={totalDutyHours}
                overtimeResult={overtimeResult}
              />
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
        <FortnightBlock
          selectedMonth={selectedMonth}
          duties={duties}
          rateOfPay={Number(rateOfPay)}
          dearnessAllowance={dearnessAllowance}
          transportationAllowance={transportationAllowance}
          totalOvertimeAllowance={overtimeResult}
          onOvertimeAllowanceChange={this.handleOvertimeAllowanceChange}
          onFortnightsChange={this.handleFortnightsChange}
        />
        {duties.length > 0 && (
          <ScrollView>
            <SummaryBlock
              totalAllowance={totalDutyAllowance}
              totalKilometers={totalKilometers}
              mileage={mileage}
              nightDutyAllowance={nightDutyAllowance}
              nationalHolidayAllowance={nationalHolidayAllowance}
              leaveMileageAllowance={leaveMileageAllowance}
              overTimeAllowance={overTimeAllowance}
              dearnessAllowance={dearnessAllowance}
              transportationAllowance={transportationAllowance}
              startDate={startDate}
              endDate={""}
              rraOsda={osdaAllowance}
              daysInMonth={daysInMonth}
              grandTotalAllowance={totalAllowance}
              overtimeResult={overtimeResult}
            />
          </ScrollView>
        )}
        <Modal
          isOpen={showModal}
          onClose={() => this.setState({ showModal: false })}
        >
          {selectedDuty && (
            <EndDutyModal
              duty={selectedDuty}
              updateDutyDetails={this.updateDutyDetails}
              closeModal={() => this.setState({ showModal: false })}
              selectedMonth={selectedMonth}
            />
          )}
        </Modal>
      </ScrollView>
    );
  }
}

File: ./components/allowancecalculation/calculateAllowanceAndRemarks.tsx
----------------------------------------
// Define a TypeScript type for the case parameters
interface CaseParameter {
  max?: number;
  factor: number;
  remark: string;
  isTimeBased?: boolean;
}

// Define a map for the case parameters by typeOfWork
const caseParameters: { [key: string]: CaseParameter | CaseParameter[] } = {
  Through: [
    { max: 120, factor: 1, remark: "considered actual value" },
    { max: 175, factor: 1.2, remark: "+20%" },
    { max: 200, factor: 1.3, remark: "+30%" },
    { max: Infinity, factor: 1.4, remark: "+40%" },
  ],
  "Light Engine/Multi": { factor: 1, remark: "cosidered actual value" },
  "Spare by Goods": { factor: 1, remark: "cosidered actual value" },
  "Sick leave": { factor: 1, remark: "+ 07:25" },
  "Casual leave": { factor: 1, remark: "+ 07:25" },
  "Paid leave": { factor: 1, remark: "+ 07:25" },
  "Maternal leave": { factor: 1, remark: "+ 07:25" },
  "Paternal leave": { factor: 1, remark: "+ 07:25" },
  Spare: { factor: 0.5, remark: "1/2" },
  Ghat: { factor: 5, remark: "*5" },
  Shunting: { factor: 0.25, remark: "+1 Hour : 15km", isTimeBased: true },
  "Light Engine on Mechanical Account": {
    factor: 0.33,
    remark: "+1hour : 20km",
    isTimeBased: true,
  },
  "Breakdown Specials": {
    factor: 0.41,
    remark: "+1hour : 25km",
    isTimeBased: true,
  },
  "Crane Specials": {
    factor: 0.33,
    remark: "+1hour : 20km",
    isTimeBased: true,
  },
  "Material Train": { factor: 1.0, remark: "+1hour : 20km", isTimeBased: true },
  "Medical Relief Trains": {
    factor: 0.41,
    remark: "+1hour : 25km",
    isTimeBased: true,
  },
  default: { factor: 1, remark: "" },
};

// TypeScript function to calculate allowance and remarks
function calculateAllowanceAndRemarks(
  distance: number,
  typeOfWork: string,
  timeInMinutes: number
): { allowance: number; remarks: string } {
  const params = caseParameters[typeOfWork] || caseParameters["default"];

  if (Array.isArray(params)) {
    for (const param of params) {
      if (distance <= param.max!) {
        return {
          allowance: parseFloat((distance * param.factor).toFixed(2)),
          remarks: param.remark,
        };
      }
    }
  } else {
    let allowance: number;
    if (params.isTimeBased) {
      // Calculate distance based on time for time-based cases
      const timeBasedConstant = getTimeBasedConstant(typeOfWork, timeInMinutes);
      const distance = (timeInMinutes / 60) * timeBasedConstant;
      allowance = parseFloat(distance.toFixed(2));
    } else {
      allowance = params.factor
        ? parseFloat((distance * params.factor).toFixed(2))
        : 0;
    }

    return {
      allowance: parseFloat(allowance.toFixed(2)),
      remarks: params.remark,
    };
  }
  return { allowance: 0, remarks: "Invalid typeOfWork or distance" }; // Fallback in case no conditions are met
}

// Helper function to get the time-based constant for the given typeOfWork
function getTimeBasedConstant(
  typeOfWork: string,
  timeInMinutes: number
): number {
  const roundedTimeInMinutes = Math.ceil(timeInMinutes / 5) * 5; // Round up to the nearest multiple of 5
  switch (typeOfWork) {
    case "Shunting":
      return 15;
    case "Light Engine on Mechanical Account":
      return 20;
    case "Breakdown Specials":
      return 25;
    case "Crane Specials":
      return 20;
    case "Material Train":
      return 20; // Special case for Material Train
    case "Medical Relief Trains":
      return 25;
    default:
      return roundedTimeInMinutes; // For other cases, return the rounded time in minutes
  }
}

export default calculateAllowanceAndRemarks;



File: ./components/allowancecalculation/leaveMileage.tsx
----------------------------------------
import Duty from "../duty/Duty";
import { leaveTypes } from "../../utils/constants";

interface CalculateLeaveMileageParams {
  rateOfPay: number;
  daysInMonth: number;
  duties: Duty[];
}

interface CalculateLeaveMileageResult {
  leaveMileageAllowance: number;
}



function calculateLeaveMileage({
  rateOfPay,
  daysInMonth,
  duties,
}: CalculateLeaveMileageParams): CalculateLeaveMileageResult {
  // Handle missing parameters
  if (rateOfPay === undefined || daysInMonth === undefined || duties === undefined) {
    throw new Error("Missing parameters");
  }

  // Handle negative values
  if (rateOfPay < 0 || daysInMonth < 0) {
    throw new Error("Negative values are not allowed");
  }

  // Handle edge case when daysInMonth is 0
  if (daysInMonth === 0) {
    throw new Error("daysInMonth cannot be zero");
  }

  // Calculate total leaves
  const totalLeaves = duties.reduce((count, duty) => {
    return count + duty.trips.filter(trip => leaveTypes.includes(trip.typeOfWork)).length;
  }, 0);


  const lmc = (0.3 * rateOfPay) / daysInMonth;
  const leaveMileageAllowance = lmc * totalLeaves;

  return { leaveMileageAllowance };
}

export default calculateLeaveMileage;

File: ./components/allowancecalculation/nightDutyAllowance.tsx
----------------------------------------
function computeNightDutyAllowance(
  rateOfPay: number | undefined,
  dearnessAllowanceValue: number | undefined,
  nightDutyHours: number | undefined
): number | undefined {
  // Check for missing values
  if (
    rateOfPay === undefined ||
    dearnessAllowanceValue === undefined ||
    nightDutyHours === undefined
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  // Check for negative values
  if (rateOfPay < 0 || dearnessAllowanceValue < 0 || nightDutyHours < 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  // Check for zero values
  if (rateOfPay === 0 && dearnessAllowanceValue === 0 && nightDutyHours === 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  // Calculate units
  const units = nightDutyHours / 6;

  // Calculate total night duty pay
  const totalNightDutyPay = ((parseFloat(rateOfPay) + dearnessAllowanceValue) / 200) * units;

  return totalNightDutyPay;
}

export default computeNightDutyAllowance;

import {DESIGNATION_MULTIPLIERS} from "../../utils/constants";

interface DesignationMultipliers {
  [key: string]: number;
}

const calculateOsdaAllowance = (
  designation: string,
  totalOsda: number
): number => {

  // Handle missing parameters
  if (designation === undefined || totalOsda === undefined) {
    throw new Error("Missing parameters");
  }

  // Handle non-numeric totalOsda values
  if (typeof totalOsda !== "number") {
    throw new Error("totalOsda must be a numeric value");
  }

  // Handle negative totalOsda values
  if (totalOsda < 0) {
    throw new Error("Negative totalOsda values are not allowed");
  }

  const multiplier: number =
    (DESIGNATION_MULTIPLIERS as DesignationMultipliers)[designation] || 1;

  return totalOsda * 70 * multiplier;
};

export default calculateOsdaAllowance;


File: ./components/allowancecalculation/overtimeAllowance.tsx
----------------------------------------
import Duty from '../duty/Duty';
import Trip from '../trip/Trip';

interface Range {
  limit: number;
  multiplier: number;
  extra: number;
}

interface FortnightData {
  startDate: Date;
  endDate: Date;
  overtimeHours: number;
  overtimeMinutes: number;
  overtimeUnit: number;
}

interface OverTimeCalculationResult {
  firstFortnight: FortnightData | null;
  secondFortnight: FortnightData | null;
}

const ranges: Range[] = [
  { limit: 104 * 60, multiplier: 1.5, extra: 0 },
  { limit: 108 * 60, multiplier: 2, extra: 6 },
];

const calculateOvertimeUnit = (overtimeTotalTime: number): number => {
  let overtimeUnit = 0;

  if (overtimeTotalTime <= ranges[0].limit) {
    overtimeUnit = 0;
  } else if (overtimeTotalTime > ranges[0].limit && overtimeTotalTime <= ranges[1].limit) {
    overtimeUnit = (overtimeTotalTime - ranges[0].limit) * ranges[0].multiplier;
  } else if (overtimeTotalTime > ranges[1].limit) {
    overtimeUnit = (overtimeTotalTime - ranges[1].limit) * ranges[1].multiplier + ranges[1].extra;
  }

  return overtimeUnit;
};

const calculateFortnight = (duties: Duty[], offsetDays: number, startDate: Date | null = null): FortnightData | null => {
  if (!duties || duties.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  if (!startDate) {
    const firstTripDate = duties[0].trips.find(trip => trip.date)?.date;
    if (!firstTripDate) {
      console.error('calculateFortnight: Invalid first trip date');
      return null;
    }
    startDate = new Date(firstTripDate);
  }

  const endDate = new Date(startDate);
  endDate.setDate(endDate.getDate() + 13 + offsetDays);

  let overtimeTotalTime = 0;
  duties.forEach(duty => {
    if (!duty.trips || duty.trips.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    const firstTripDate = new Date(duty.trips[0].date);
    if (isNaN(firstTripDate.getTime()) || firstTripDate < startDate || firstTripDate >= endDate) {
      return;
    }

    const overtimeForDuty = duty.dutyHours * 60 ;

    overtimeTotalTime += overtimeForDuty;
  });

  const fortnightData: FortnightData = {
    startDate: startDate,
    endDate: endDate,
    overtimeHours: Math.floor(overtimeTotalTime / 60),
    overtimeMinutes: overtimeTotalTime % 60,
    overtimeUnit: calculateOvertimeUnit(overtimeTotalTime)
  };

  return fortnightData;
};

const firstFortnight = (duties: Duty[], startDate: Date): FortnightData | null => {
  return calculateFortnight(duties, 0);
};

const secondFortnight = (duties: Duty[], firstFortnightEndDate: Date | null): FortnightData | null => {
  if (!firstFortnightEndDate || !(firstFortnightEndDate instanceof Date)) {
    return null;
  }
  const newStartDate = new Date(firstFortnightEndDate);
  newStartDate.setDate(firstFortnightEndDate.getDate() + 1);
  return calculateFortnight(duties, 0, newStartDate);
};

const overtimeCalculation = (duties: Duty[], startDate: Date): OverTimeCalculationResult => {
  
    const firstFortnightData = firstFortnight(duties, startDate);
  
    const firstFortnightEndDate = firstFortnightData ? new Date(firstFortnightData.endDate) : null;
  
    const secondFortnightData = secondFortnight(duties, firstFortnightEndDate);
  
    return {
      firstFortnight: firstFortnightData,
      secondFortnight: secondFortnightData
    };
  };

export { overtimeCalculation, calculateOvertimeUnit, calculateFortnight, firstFortnight, secondFortnight };

File: ./components/time-picker/TimePicker.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity } from 'react-native';
import { Picker } from "@react-native-picker/picker";
import DateTimePicker from '@react-native-community/datetimepicker';

interface TimePickerProps {
  onTimeChange: (hour: string, minute: string) => void;
  disabled: boolean;
}

interface TimePickerState {
  selectedHour: string | null;
  selectedMinute: string | null;
  showTimePicker: boolean;
}

class TimePicker extends React.Component<TimePickerProps, TimePickerState> {
  constructor(props: TimePickerProps) {
    super(props);
    this.state = {
      selectedHour: null,
      selectedMinute: null,
      showTimePicker: false,
    };
  }

  handleHourChange = (itemValue: string) => {
    this.setState({ selectedHour: itemValue }, () => {
      if (itemValue !== '-') {
        this.props.onTimeChange(itemValue, this.state.selectedMinute || '');
      }
    });
  };

  handleMinuteChange = (itemValue: string) => {
    this.setState({ selectedMinute: itemValue }, () => {
      if (itemValue !== '-') {
        this.props.onTimeChange(this.state.selectedHour || '', itemValue);
      }
    });
  };

  handleTimeChange = (event: any, selectedTime?: Date) => {
    if (event.type === 'set') {
      const hour = String(selectedTime?.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
      const minute = String(selectedTime?.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
      this.setState(
        {
          selectedHour: hour,
          selectedMinute: minute,
          showTimePicker: false,
        },
        () => {
          this.props.onTimeChange(hour, minute);
        }
      );
    } else {
      this.setState({ showTimePicker: false });
    }
  };

  openTimePicker = () => {
    this.setState({ showTimePicker: true });
  };

  render() {
    const { disabled } = this.props;
    const { selectedHour, selectedMinute, showTimePicker } = this.state;

    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      return (
        <View style={styles.container}>
          <Picker
            selectedValue={selectedHour}
            style={styles.picker}
            disabled={disabled}
            onValueChange={this.handleHourChange}
          >
            <Picker.Item label="-" value="-" />
            {Array.from({ length: 24 }).map((_, index) => (
              <Picker.Item
                key={index}
                label={String(index).padStart(2, '0')}
                value={String(index).padStart(2, '0')}
              />
            ))}
          </Picker>
          <Picker
            selectedValue={selectedMinute}
            style={styles.picker1}
            onValueChange={this.handleMinuteChange}
            disabled={disabled}
          >
            <Picker.Item label="-" value="-" />
            {Array.from({ length: 60 }).map((_, index) => (
              <Picker.Item
                key={index}
                label={String(index).padStart(2, '0')}
                value={String(index).padStart(2, '0')}
              />
            ))}
          </Picker>
        </View>
      );
    }

    return (
      <View>
        <View style={styles.container}>
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={this.openTimePicker} disabled={disabled}>
            <View style={styles.timeContainer}>
              <View>
                <Text>{selectedHour || '--'}</Text>
              </View>
              <Text>:</Text>
              <View>
                <Text>{selectedMinute || '--'}</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
        {showTimePicker && (
          <DateTimePicker
            value={new Date()}
            mode="time"
            is24Hour={true}
            display="default"
            onChange={this.handleTimeChange}
          />
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  picker: {
    flex: 1,
    margin: 1,
    height: 25,
  },
  picker1: {
    flex: 1,
    margin: 1,
    height: 25,
  },
  timeContainer: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    padding: 10,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: 'gray',
  },
});

export default TimePicker;


File: ./components/station-picker/stationPicker.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, Platform } from "react-native";
import { Picker } from "@react-native-picker/picker";
import { combinations } from "../../utils/combinations";
import Duty from "../duty/Duty";

interface PickerComponentStationProps {
  onStationChange: (
    dutyId: number,
    tripId: number,
    sourceStation: string,
    destinationStation: string,
    distance: number
  ) => void;
  dutyId: number;
  tripId: number;
  disabled: boolean;
  duty: Duty;
}

const PickerComponentStation: React.FC<PickerComponentStationProps> = ({
  onStationChange,
  dutyId,
  tripId,
  disabled,
  duty,
}) => {
  const [fromStation, setFromStation] = useState("");
  const [toStation, setToStation] = useState("");

  const currentTripIndex = duty.trips.findIndex((trip) => trip.id === tripId);
  const previousTrip =
    currentTripIndex > 0 ? duty.trips[currentTripIndex - 1] : null;
  const previousTripDestinationStation = previousTrip
    ? previousTrip.destinationStation
    : "";

  useEffect(() => {
    setFromStation(previousTripDestinationStation || "");
  }, [previousTripDestinationStation]);

  const handleFromStationChange = (selectedStation: string) => {
    setFromStation(selectedStation);
    updateToStationAndDistance(selectedStation, toStation);
  };

  const handleToStationChange = (selectedStation: string) => {
    setToStation(selectedStation);
    updateToStationAndDistance(fromStation, selectedStation);
  };

  const updateToStationAndDistance = (from: string, to: string) => {
    const combo = combinations.find(
      (c) => c.from_station === from && c.to_station === to
    );
    if (combo) {
      onStationChange(dutyId, tripId, from, to, combo.distance);
    }
  };

  const uniqueFromStations = Array.from(
    new Set(combinations.map((item) => item.from_station))
  );

  const renderPicker = (isFrom: boolean) => {
    const label = isFrom ? "From:" : "To:";
    const value = isFrom ? fromStation : toStation;
    const onValueChange = isFrom ? handleFromStationChange : handleToStationChange;
    const items = isFrom
      ? uniqueFromStations
      : combinations.filter((item) => item.from_station === fromStation).map((item) => item.to_station);

    return (
      <View style={styles.pickerContainer}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>{label}</Text>
        {Platform.OS === "web" ? (
          <select
            value={value}
            onChange={(e) => onValueChange(e.target.value)}
            disabled={disabled}
            style={styles.webSelect}
          >
            <option value="">{isFrom ? "From Station" : "To Station"}</option>
            {items.map((station, index) => (
              <option key={index} value={station}>
                {station}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        ) : (
          <Picker
            selectedValue={value}
            onValueChange={onValueChange}
            enabled={!disabled}
            style={styles.picker}
          >
            <Picker.Item label={isFrom ? "From Station" : "To Station"} value="" />
            {items.map((station, index) => (
              <Picker.Item key={index} label={station} value={station} />
            ))}
          </Picker>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {renderPicker(true)}
      {renderPicker(false)}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    width: "100%",
  },
  pickerContainer: {
    marginBottom: 4,
  },
  label: {
    fontSize: 10,
    marginBottom: 2,
  },
  picker: {
    height: 30,
    backgroundColor: "white",
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#ccc",
    borderRadius: 4,
  },
  webSelect: {
    width: "100%",
    height: 30,
    backgroundColor: "white",
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#ccc",
    borderRadius: 4,
    fontSize: 12,
  },
});

export default PickerComponentStation;


File: ./components/DatePicker/DatePickerComponent.tsx

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Platform,
} from "react-native";
import DateTimePickerModal from "react-native-modal-datetime-picker";

interface DatePickerComponentProps {
  onDateSelected: (date: string) => void;
  defaultDate: string;
  minimumDate?: Date; // Add the minimumDate prop
}

const DatePickerComponent: React.FC<DatePickerComponentProps> = ({
  onDateSelected,
  defaultDate,
  minimumDate, // Add the minimumDate prop
}) => {
  const [isDatePickerVisible, setDatePickerVisible] = useState(false);
  const [selectedDateString, setSelectedDateString] = useState("Select Date");

  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState(new Date(defaultDate)); // Set initial date to defaultDate

  const handleConfirm = (date: Date) => {
    const formattedDate = `${date.getFullYear()}-${(date.getMonth() + 1)
      .toString()
      .padStart(2, "0")}-${date.getDate().toString().padStart(2, "0")}`;
    setSelectedDate(date);
    setSelectedDateString(formattedDate);
    onDateSelected(formattedDate);
    setDatePickerVisible(false);
  };

  const showDatePicker = () => {
    setDatePickerVisible(true);
  };

  const hideDatePicker = () => {
    setDatePickerVisible(false);
  };

  return (
    <View style={{ borderWidth: 1, padding: 4 }}>
      <TouchableOpacity onPress={showDatePicker}>
        <Text>{selectedDateString}</Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>
      <DateTimePickerModal
        isVisible={isDatePickerVisible}
        mode="date"
        onConfirm={handleConfirm}
        onCancel={hideDatePicker}
        isDarkModeEnabled={Platform.OS === "ios"} // Example of platform-specific property
        date={selectedDate} // Set initial date to selectedDate
        minimumDate={minimumDate} // Set the minimum selectable date
      />
    </View>
  );
};

export default DatePickerComponent;

File: ./components/summary-block/SummaryBlock.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React from "react";
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, ScrollView, Dimensions } from "react-native";

interface OvertimeCalculationResult {
  firstFortnight?: {
    startDate: string;
    endDate: string;
    overtimeHours: number;
    overtimeMinutes: number;
    overtimeUnit: string;
  };
  secondFortnight?: {
    startDate: string;
    endDate: string;
    overtimeHours: number;
    overtimeMinutes: number;
    overtimeUnit: string;
  };
}

interface SummaryBlockProps {
  totalAllowance: number | undefined;
  totalKilometers: number | undefined;
  mileage: number | undefined;
  nightDutyAllowance: number | undefined;
  nationalHolidayAllowance: number | undefined;
  leaveMileageAllowance: number | undefined;
  rraOsda: number | undefined;
  overTimeAllowance: number | undefined;
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  dearnessAllowance: number;
  transportationAllowance: number;
  grandTotalAllowance: number | undefined;
  overtimeResult: OvertimeCalculationResult | null;
}

const SummaryBlock: React.FC<SummaryBlockProps> = ({
  totalAllowance,
  totalKilometers,
  mileage,
  nightDutyAllowance,
  nationalHolidayAllowance,
  leaveMileageAllowance,
  rraOsda,
  overTimeAllowance,
  startDate,
  endDate,
  dearnessAllowance,
  transportationAllowance,
  grandTotalAllowance,
  overtimeResult,
}) => {
  const formatNumber = (value: number | undefined) => {
    return value !== undefined ? value.toFixed(2) : "-";
  };

  const formatDate = (dateString: string) => {
    const date = new Date(dateString);
    return date.toLocaleDateString();
  };

  // Helper function to convert decimal hours to HH:MM format
  const formatHoursToHHMM = (hours: number) => {
    const wholeHours = Math.floor(hours);
    const decimalHours = hours - wholeHours;
    const minutes = Math.round(decimalHours * 60);
    return `${wholeHours.toString().padStart(2, "0")}:${minutes
      .toString()
      .padStart(2, "0")}`;
  };
  const formatOvertimeDuration = (hours: number, minutes: number) => {
    const totalMinutes = hours * 60 + minutes;
    const formattedHours = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60)
      .toString()
      .padStart(2, "0");
    const remainingMinutes = totalMinutes % 60;
    const formattedMinutes = remainingMinutes
      .toFixed(2)
      .padStart(4, "0")
      .slice(0, 2);
    return `${formattedHours}:${formattedMinutes}`;
  };
  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.headerContainer}>
        <Text style={styles.heading}>Summary:</Text>
      </View>
      <ScrollView horizontal contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}>
        <View style={styles.infoContainerWrapper}>
          <View style={styles.infoContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Dearness Allowance:</Text>
            <Text style={styles.value}>₹{formatNumber(dearnessAllowance)}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.infoContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Transportation Allowance:</Text>
            <Text style={styles.value}>₹{formatNumber(transportationAllowance)}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.infoContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Kilometers Earned:</Text>
            <Text style={styles.value}>{formatNumber(totalAllowance)}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.infoContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Mileage:</Text>
            <Text style={styles.value}>₹{formatNumber(mileage)}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.label}>multiplier*km </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.infoContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Night Duty:</Text>
            <Text style={styles.value}>
              ₹{formatNumber(nightDutyAllowance)}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.infoContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Leave Mileage:</Text>
            <Text style={styles.value}>
              ₹{formatNumber(leaveMileageAllowance)}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.infoContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>RRA / OSDA:</Text>
            <Text style={styles.value}>₹{formatNumber(rraOsda)}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.infoContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Total Allowance:</Text>
            <Text style={styles.value}>
              ₹{formatNumber(grandTotalAllowance)}
            </Text>
          </View>
          {/* <View style={styles.infoContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Over Time:</Text>
            <Text style={styles.value}>₹{formatNumber(overTimeAllowance)}</Text>
          </View> */}
        </View>
        {overtimeResult && (
          <View style={{ flexDirection: "row" }}>
            {overtimeResult.firstFortnight && (
              <View style={styles.overtimeRow}>
                <View style={styles.overtimeInfo}>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>First Fortnight</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.value}>
                    {formatOvertimeDuration(
                      overtimeResult.firstFortnight.overtimeHours,
                      overtimeResult.firstFortnight.overtimeMinutes
                    )}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.overtimeUnit}>
                    ({overtimeResult.firstFortnight.overtimeUnit})
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.overtimeDates}>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>Start Date:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.value}>
                    {formatDate(overtimeResult.firstFortnight.startDate)}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>End Date:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.value}>
                    {formatDate(overtimeResult.firstFortnight.endDate)}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            )}
            {overtimeResult.secondFortnight && (
              <View style={styles.overtimeRow}>
                <View style={styles.overtimeInfo}>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>Second Fortnight</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.value}>
                    {formatOvertimeDuration(
                      overtimeResult.secondFortnight.overtimeHours,
                      overtimeResult.secondFortnight.overtimeMinutes
                    )}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.overtimeUnit}>
                    ({overtimeResult.secondFortnight.overtimeUnit})
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.overtimeDates}>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>Start Date:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.value}>
                    {formatDate(overtimeResult.secondFortnight.startDate)}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>End Date:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.value}>
                    {formatDate(overtimeResult.secondFortnight.endDate)}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    backgroundColor: "#f0f0f0",
    borderRadius: 8,
    marginVertical: 1,
    // maxHeight: Dimensions.get("window").height * 0.3, // Increased maxHeight
  },
  headerContainer: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    // justifyContent: "space-between",
    // alignItems: "center",
    // paddingHorizontal: 2,
    marginLeft: 2,
    paddingTop: 8,
  },
  heading: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: "bold",
    marginLeft: 5,
  },
  dates: {
    fontSize: 12,
  },
  scrollContent: {
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingBottom: 8,
  },
  infoContainerWrapper: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    flexWrap: "wrap",
  },
  infoContainer: {
    marginRight: 24,
    alignItems: "flex-start",
  },
  overtimeContainer: {
    maxHeight: "30%", // Added maxHeight
    flexDirection: "row",
  },
  overtimeRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    // justifyContent: "space-between",
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    // paddingVertical: 1,
  },
  overtimeInfo: {
    alignItems: "flex-start",
  },

    fontSize: 12,
  },

// InterdependentPicker.tsx
import React from "react";
import { View, Platform } from "react-native";
import { Picker } from "@react-native-picker/picker";
import { levelOptions,payBandOptions,rateOfPayOptions } from "../utils/constants";

interface InterdependentPickersProps {
  payBand: string;
  setPayBand: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>;
  level: string;
  setLevel: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>;
  rateOfPay: string;
  setRateOfPay: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>;
}

const InterdependentPickers: React.FC<InterdependentPickersProps> = ({
  payBand,
  setPayBand,
  level,
  setLevel,
  rateOfPay,
  setRateOfPay,
}) => {
 
  return (
    <View style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "center" }}>
      <View
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          ...(Platform.OS === "ios" && {
            overflow: "hidden",
            justifyContent: "center",
          }),
          width: 350,
          height: 60,
        }}
      >
        <Picker
          style={{
            height: 40,
            ...(Platform.OS === "ios" && { marginBottom: 180 }),
          }}
          selectedValue={payBand}
          onValueChange={(value) => {
            setPayBand(value);
            setLevel("");
            setRateOfPay("");
          }}
        >
          <Picker.Item label="Select Pay Band" value="" />
          {payBandOptions.map((option) => (
            <Picker.Item key={option} label={option} value={option} />
          ))}
        </Picker>
      </View>
      <View
        style={{
          ...(Platform.OS === "ios" && {
            overflow: "hidden",
            justifyContent: "center",
          }),
          display: "flex",
          width: 350,
          marginTop: 20,
          height: 60,
        }}
      >
        <Picker
          style={{
            height: 40,
            ...(Platform.OS === "ios" && { marginBottom: 180 }),
          }}
          selectedValue={level}
          onValueChange={(value) => {
            setLevel(value);
            setRateOfPay("");
          }}
        >
          <Picker.Item label="Select Level of Pay" value="" />
          {(levelOptions[payBand] || []).map((option) => (
            <Picker.Item key={option} label={option} value={option} />
          ))}
        </Picker>
      </View>
      <View
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          ...(Platform.OS === "ios" && {
            overflow: "hidden",
            justifyContent: "center",
          }),
          width: 350,
          marginTop: 20,
          height: 60,
        }}
      >
        <Picker
          style={{
            height: 40,
            ...(Platform.OS === "ios" && { marginBottom: 180 }),
          }}
          selectedValue={rateOfPay}
          onValueChange={setRateOfPay}
        >
          <Picker.Item label="Select Rate of Pay" value="" />
          {(rateOfPayOptions[level] || []).map((option, index) => (
            <Picker.Item
              key={index}
              label={option.toString()}
              value={option.toString()}
            />
          ))}
        </Picker>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

export default InterdependentPickers;

// Fortnight.ts
export default class Fortnight {
  startDate: Date;
  endDate: Date;
  totalForthnightDuration: number;
  overtimeAllowance: number;
  totalOvertimeHours: number;

  constructor(
    startDate: Date,
    endDate: Date,
    totalForthnightDuration: number,
    overtimeAllowance: number,
    totalOvertimeHours: number
  ) {
    this.startDate = startDate;
    this.endDate = endDate;
    this.totalForthnightDuration = totalForthnightDuration;
    this.overtimeAllowance = overtimeAllowance;
    this.totalOvertimeHours = totalOvertimeHours;
  }
}


File: ./components/fortnight-block/FortnightBlock.tsx
----------------------------------------
// FortnightBlock.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TextInput,
  Button,
  ScrollView,
} from "react-native";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import Duty from "../duty/Duty";
import Fortnight from "./Fortnight";

interface FortnightBlockProps {
  selectedMonth: string;
  duties: Duty[];
  rateOfPay: number;
  dearnessAllowance: number;
  transportationAllowance: number;
  totalOvertimeAllowance: number;
  onOvertimeAllowanceChange: (newAllowance: number) => void;
  onFortnightsChange: (fortnights: Fortnight[]) => void;
}

const FORTNIGHT_DATES = [
  new Date("2024-01-01"),
  new Date("2024-01-15"),
  new Date("2024-01-29"),
  new Date("2024-02-12"),
  new Date("2024-02-26"),
  new Date("2024-03-11"),
  new Date("2024-03-25"),
  new Date("2024-04-08"),
  new Date("2024-04-22"),
  new Date("2024-05-06"),
  new Date("2024-05-19"),
  new Date("2024-06-02"),
  new Date("2024-06-16"),
  new Date("2024-06-30"),
  new Date("2024-07-14"),
  new Date("2024-07-28"),
  new Date("2024-08-11"),
  new Date("2024-08-25"),
  new Date("2024-09-08"),
  new Date("2024-09-22"),
  new Date("2024-10-06"),
  new Date("2024-10-20"),
  new Date("2024-11-03"),
  new Date("2024-11-17"),
  new Date("2024-12-01"),
  new Date("2024-12-15"),
  new Date("2024-12-29"),
];

const FortnightBlock: React.FC<FortnightBlockProps> = ({
  selectedMonth,
  duties,
  rateOfPay,
  dearnessAllowance,
  transportationAllowance,
  totalOvertimeAllowance,
  onOvertimeAllowanceChange,
  onFortnightsChange,
}) => {
  const [fortnights, setFortnights] = useState<Fortnight[]>([]);
  const [pastFortnightHours, setPastFortnightHours] = useState(0);
  const [pastFortnightMinutes, setPastFortnightMinutes] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const pastFortnight = pastFortnightHours + pastFortnightMinutes / 60;
    recalculateFortnights(pastFortnight);
    onFortnightsChange(fortnights);
  }, [selectedMonth, duties, pastFortnightHours, pastFortnightMinutes]);

  const recalculateFortnights = (pastFortnight: number) => {
    const monthIndex = new Date(
      Date.parse(`${selectedMonth} 1, 2024`)
    ).getMonth();
    const startDate = new Date(2024, monthIndex, 1);
    const endDate = new Date(2024, monthIndex + 1, 0);

      // Sort duties by start date in ascending order
  const sortedDuties = [...duties].sort((a, b) => {
    const aStartDate = a.signIn ? new Date(a.signIn) : new Date(a.trips[0].date);
    const bStartDate = b.signIn ? new Date(b.signIn) : new Date(b.trips[0].date);
    return aStartDate.getTime() - bStartDate.getTime();
  });

    const intersectingFortnights: Fortnight[] = FORTNIGHT_DATES.filter(
      (date) => {
        const fortnightEnd = new Date(date);
        fortnightEnd.setDate(fortnightEnd.getDate() + 13);
        return date <= endDate && fortnightEnd >= startDate;
      }
    ).map((startDate, index) => {
      const endDate = new Date(startDate.getTime() + 13 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

      let totalForthnightDuration = index === 0 ? pastFortnight : 0;

      sortedDuties.forEach((duty) => {
        let dutyStart;
        if (duty.isLeave && duty.trips.length > 0) {
          dutyStart = new Date(duty.trips[0].date);
        } else {
          dutyStart = new Date(duty.signIn);
        }
        if (dutyStart >= startDate && dutyStart <= endDate) {
          const dutyHours =
            duty.dutyHours && !isNaN(duty.dutyHours)
              ? parseFloat(duty.dutyHours)
              : 0;
          totalForthnightDuration += dutyHours;
          // if (!duty.isLeave) {
          duty.progressiveDutyHours = totalForthnightDuration;
        }
      });

      const basicPlusDAPlusTA =
        rateOfPay +
        dearnessAllowance +
        transportationAllowance +
        rateOfPay * 0.3;
      const lowerRate = (basicPlusDAPlusTA * 1.5) / 223;
      const higherRate = (basicPlusDAPlusTA * 2) / 223;

      let overtimeAllowance = 0;
      let totalOvertimeHours = 0;
  
      if (totalForthnightDuration > 104) {
        const overtimeHours = totalForthnightDuration - 104;
  
        if (overtimeHours <= 4) {
          overtimeAllowance = overtimeHours * lowerRate;
        } else {
          overtimeAllowance = 4 * lowerRate + (overtimeHours - 4) * higherRate;
        }
  
        totalOvertimeHours = overtimeHours;
      }

      return new Fortnight(
        startDate,
        endDate,
        totalForthnightDuration,
        overtimeAllowance,
        totalOvertimeHours
      );
    });

    const totalOvertimeAllowance = intersectingFortnights.reduce(
      (total, fortnight) => total + fortnight.overtimeAllowance,
      0
    );

    setFortnights(intersectingFortnights);
    onOvertimeAllowanceChange(totalOvertimeAllowance);
  };

  // const handleApply = () => {
  //   const hours = Number(inputHours);
  //   setInputHours(hours);
  // };

  const handlePastFortnightHoursChange = (text: string) => {
    const hours = parseInt(text, 10);
    if (!isNaN(hours) && hours >= 0 && hours <= 200) {
      setPastFortnightHours(hours);
    } else {
      setPastFortnightHours(0);
    }
  };

  const handlePastFortnightMinutesChange = (text: string) => {
    const minutes = parseInt(text, 10);
    if (!isNaN(minutes) && minutes >= 0 && minutes <= 59) {
      setPastFortnightMinutes(minutes);
    } else {
      setPastFortnightMinutes(0);
    }
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <Text style={styles.title}>Fortnights in {selectedMonth}</Text>
      <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
        <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Past Fortnight Duration</Text>
        <View style={styles.durationInputContainer}>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.durationInput}
            value={pastFortnightHours.toString()}
            keyboardType="numeric"
            onChangeText={handlePastFortnightHoursChange}
            maxLength={3}
          />
          <Text style={styles.durationSeparator}>:</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.durationInput}
            value={pastFortnightMinutes.toString()}
            keyboardType="numeric"
            onChangeText={handlePastFortnightMinutesChange}
            maxLength={2}
          />
        </View>
      </View>
      <ScrollView horizontal contentContainerStyle={styles.fortnightsContainer}>
        {fortnights.map((fortnight, index) => (
          <View key={index} style={styles.fortnight}>
            <Text style={styles.fortnightText}>
              {format(fortnight.startDate, "dd MMM")} -{" "}
              {format(fortnight.endDate, "dd MMM")}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.fortnightText}>
              Total Hours:{" "}
              {fortnight.totalForthnightDuration
                ? fortnight.totalForthnightDuration.toFixed(2)
                : "0.00"}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.fortnightText}>
              Overtime Hours: {fortnight.totalOvertimeHours.toFixed(2)}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.fortnightText}>
              Overtime Allowance:{" "}
              {fortnight.overtimeAllowance
                ? fortnight.overtimeAllowance.toFixed(2)
                : "0.00"}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
      <Text style={styles.totalText}>
        Total Overtime Allowance:{" "}
        {totalOvertimeAllowance ? totalOvertimeAllowance.toFixed(2) : "0.00"}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    padding: 16,
    backgroundColor: "#f9f9f9",
    borderRadius: 8,
    marginVertical: 16,
  },
  title: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: "bold",
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  fortnightText: {
    fontSize: 14,
  },
  inputContainer: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    marginBottom: 16,
  },
  inputLabel: {
    fontSize: 14,
    marginRight: 8,
  },
  input: {
    height: 40,
    borderColor: "#ccc",
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 4,
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    marginRight: 8,
  },
  totalText: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: "bold",
    marginTop: 16,
  },
  fortnightsContainer: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    marginBottom: 16,
  },
  fortnight: {
    minWidth: 120,
    padding: 8,
    backgroundColor: "#ffffff",
    borderRadius: 4,
    marginRight: 8,
  },
  durationInputContainer: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  durationInput: {
    width: 40,
    height: 40,
    borderColor: "#ccc",
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 4,
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    marginRight: 4,
    textAlign: "center",
  },
  durationSeparator: {
    fontSize: 18,
    marginHorizontal: 4,
  },
});

export default FortnightBlock;



File: ./components/type-of-work/typeofwork.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React, { SetStateAction, useState } from "react";
import { View, Platform, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import { Picker } from "@react-native-picker/picker";
import RNPickerSelect from "react-native-picker-select";
import { pickerItems } from "../../utils/constants";

interface TypeofWorkPickerComponentProps {
  TypeofWork: (newValue: string) => void;
}

const TypeofWorkPickerComponent: React.FC<TypeofWorkPickerComponentProps> = ({
  TypeofWork,
}) => {
  const [ChoosenItem, setChoosenItem] = useState("");

  const commonPickerStyle = {
    height: 40,
    width: "100%",
    backgroundColor: "white",
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#ccc",
    borderRadius: 5,
  };

  // iOS-specific picker using RNPickerSelect
  const iosPicker = (
    <RNPickerSelect
      value={ChoosenItem}
      onValueChange={(newValue: SetStateAction<string>) => {
        setChoosenItem(newValue);
        TypeofWork(newValue);
      }}
      items={pickerItems.map((item) => ({
        label: item.label,
        value: item.value,
      }))}
      placeholder={{
        label: "Select Duty Type",
        value: "Select Duty Type",
        color: "#9EA0A4",
      }}
      style={{
        inputIOS: {
          ...commonPickerStyle,
          paddingHorizontal: 10,
        },
      }}
    />
  );

  // Android and potentially web picker using the default Picker
  const defaultPicker = (
    <Picker
      selectedValue={ChoosenItem}
      onValueChange={(newValue) => {
        setChoosenItem(newValue);
        TypeofWork(newValue);
      }}
      style={commonPickerStyle}
    >
      <Picker.Item label="Duty type" value="Select Duty Type" />
      {pickerItems.map((item, index) => (
        <Picker.Item key={index} label={item.label} value={item.value} />
      ))}
    </Picker>
  );

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {Platform.OS === "ios" ? iosPicker : defaultPicker}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    width: "100%",
  },
});

export default TypeofWorkPickerComponent;

interface PdfGeneratorMobileProps {

  payBand: string;
  level: string;
  rateOfPay: number;
  selectedMonth: string;
  totalKilometers: number;
  mileage: number;
  nightDutyAllowance: number;
  leaveMileageAllowance: number;
  dearnessAllowance: number;

              <th>Type of Work</th>
              <th>Train No.</th>
              <th>Dep. Time</th>
              <th>Arr. Time</th>
              <th>Source</th>
              <th>Dest.</th>
              <th>Distance</th>
              <th>Duty Hours</th>
              <th>Allowance</th>
              <th>Remarks</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            ${duty.trips.map((trip) => `
              <tr>
                <td>${trip.date}</td>
                <td>${trip.typeOfWork}</td>
                <td>${trip.trainNumber}</td>
                <td>${trip.departureTime}</td>
                <td>${trip.arrivalTime}</td>
                <td>${trip.sourceStation}</td>
                <td>${trip.destinationStation}</td>
                <td>${trip.distance}</td>
                <td>${trip.dutyHours}</td>
                <td>${trip.allowance}</td>
                <td>${trip.remarks}</td>
              </tr>
            `).join('')}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th colspan="2">Duty Info</th>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <th>Sign In</th>
              <th>Sign Off</th>
              <th>Total Allowance</th>
              <th>Total Distance</th>
              <th>Duty Hours</th>
              <th>Night Duty Hours</th>
              <th>Progressive Duty Hours</th>
              <th>OSDA</th>
              <th>RRA</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr>
              <td>${duty.signIn ? duty.signIn.toLocaleString() : '-'}</td>
              <td>${duty.signOff ? duty.signOff.toLocaleString() : '-'}</td>
              <td>₹${duty.calculateTotalAllowance().toFixed(2)}</td>
              <td>${duty.calculateTotalDistance().toFixed(2)} km</td>
              <td>${duty.dutyHours.toFixed(2)} H</td>
              <td>${duty.nightDutyHours.toFixed(2)} H</td>
              <td>${duty.progressiveDutyHours.toFixed(2)} H</td>
              <td>${duty.isOSDA ? 'Yes' : 'No'}</td>
              <td>${duty.isRRA ? 'Yes' : 'No'}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      `).join('')}

      <h2>Summary Block</h2>
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Total Kilometers</th>
            <th>Mileage</th>
            <th>Night Duty Allowance</th>
            <th>Leave Mileage Allowance</th>
            <th>Dearness Allowance</th>
            <th>Progressive Duty Hours</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr>
            <td>${totalKilometers.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td>₹${mileage.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td>₹${nightDutyAllowance.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td>₹${leaveMileageAllowance.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td>₹${dearnessAllowance.toFixed(2)}</td>
            <td>${progressiveDutyHours.toFixed(2)} H</td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

        ${overtimeResult ? `
          <h2>Overtime Calculation Result</h2>
          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th colspan="2">First Fortnight</th>
              </tr>
              <tr>
                <th>Start Date</th>
                <th>End Date</th>
                <th>Overtime Hours</th>
                <th>Overtime Minutes</th>
                <th>Overtime Unit</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              <tr>
                <td>${overtimeResult.firstFortnight ? overtimeResult.firstFortnight.startDate.toDateString() : '-'}</td>
                <td>${overtimeResult.firstFortnight ? overtimeResult.firstFortnight.endDate.toDateString() : '-'}</td>
                <td>${overtimeResult.firstFortnight ? overtimeResult.firstFortnight.overtimeHours : '-'}</td>
                <td>${overtimeResult.firstFortnight ? overtimeResult.firstFortnight.overtimeMinutes : '-'}</td>
                <td>${overtimeResult.firstFortnight ? overtimeResult.firstFortnight.overtimeUnit : '-'}</td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th colspan="2">Second Fortnight</th>
              </tr>
              <tr>
                <th>Start Date</th>
                <th>End Date</th>
                <th>Overtime Hours</th>
                <th>Overtime Minutes</th>
                <th>Overtime Unit</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              <tr>
                <td>${overtimeResult.secondFortnight ? overtimeResult.secondFortnight.startDate.toDateString() : '-'}</td>
                <td>${overtimeResult.secondFortnight ? overtimeResult.secondFortnight.endDate.toDateString() : '-'}</td>
                <td>${overtimeResult.secondFortnight ? overtimeResult.secondFortnight.overtimeHours : '-'}</td>
                <td>${overtimeResult.secondFortnight ? overtimeResult.secondFortnight.overtimeMinutes : '-'}</td>
                <td>${overtimeResult.secondFortnight ? overtimeResult.secondFortnight.overtimeUnit : '-'}</td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
        ` : ''}
      </body>
    </html>
  `;
    const { uri } = await Print.printToFileAsync({
      html,
      orientation: 'landscape'

    });
    // Share the PDF file with the user
    await Sharing.shareAsync(uri, {
      mimeType: 'application/pdf',
      dialogTitle: 'Share PDF',
      UTI: 'com.adobe.pdf',
    });
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <Button title="Generate PDF" onPress={generatePDF} />
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flex: 1,
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',}});

// PDFGenerator.tsx
import React from "react";
import jsPDF from "jspdf";
import "jspdf-autotable";
import Duty from "../duty/Duty";
import Trip from "../trip/Trip";
import DutyInfo from "../duty/DutyInfo";
import { Button, View } from "react-native";
import Fortnight from "../fortnight-block/Fortnight";

// Helper function to convert hours to HH:MM format
const formatHours = (hours) => {
  const totalMinutes = Math.round(hours * 60);
  const formattedHours = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60)
    .toString()
    .padStart(2, "0");
  const formattedMinutes = (totalMinutes % 60).toString().padStart(2, "0");
  return `${formattedHours}:${formattedMinutes}`;
};

const formatDutyHours = (dutyHours) => {
  const [hours, minutes] = dutyHours.split("h ");
  const formattedHours = hours.trim().padStart(2, "0");
  const formattedMinutes = minutes
    ? minutes.trim().replace("m", "").padStart(2, "0")
    : "00";
  return `${formattedHours}:${formattedMinutes}`;
};

interface PDFGeneratorProps {
  duties: Duty[];
  designation: string;
  payBand: string;
  level: string;
  rateOfPay: number;
  selectedMonth: string;
  mileage: number;
  nightDutyAllowance: number;
  nationalHolidayAllowance: number;
  leaveMileageAllowance: number;
  overTimeAllowance: number;
  dearnessAllowance: number;
  progressiveDutyHours: number;
  grandTotalAllowance: number;
  rraOsda: number;
  fortnights: Fortnight[];
}

const PDFGenerator: React.FC<PDFGeneratorProps> = ({
  duties,
  designation,
  payBand,
  level,
  rateOfPay,
  selectedMonth,
  mileage,
  nightDutyAllowance,
  nationalHolidayAllowance,
  leaveMileageAllowance,
  overTimeAllowance,
  grandTotalAllowance,
  dearnessAllowance,
  progressiveDutyHours,
  rraOsda,
  fortnights,
}) => {
  const generatePDF = () => {
    const doc = new jsPDF(); // Set portrait orientation (default)
    const footerText =
      "This is generated by Allowance Calculator an Platform by Feyntech Optimization Solutions Pvt. Ltd.";

    // Add header row
    const headerData = [
      {
        content: "Designation",
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: designation,
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: " | ",
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: "Pay Band",
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {

        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: " | ",
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: "Level",
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: level,
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: " | ",
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: "Rate of Pay",
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: rateOfPay,
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: " | ",
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: "Selected Month",
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
      {
        content: selectedMonth,
        colSpan: 1,
        styles: {
          fontStyle: "bold",
          fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
          textColor: [0, 0, 0],
        },
      },
    ];

    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.text(
      footerText,
      doc.internal.pageSize.getWidth() -
        doc.getStringUnitWidth(footerText) -
        10,
      doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight() - 10,
      { align: "right" }
    );

    // Render header row
    doc.autoTable({
      head: [headerData],
      theme: "grid",
      styles: { fontSize: 8, halign: "left" },
      margin: { top: 10, bottom: 10 },
    });

    let currentYPosition = doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 10; // Track the current y position

    // Sort duties based on the date of the first trip in each duty
    const sortedDuties = [...duties].sort((a, b) => {
      const aFirstTripDate = a.trips.length > 0 ? new Date(a.trips[0].date).getTime() : 0;
      const bFirstTripDate = b.trips.length > 0 ? new Date(b.trips[0].date).getTime() : 0;
      return aFirstTripDate - bFirstTripDate;
    });

    sortedDuties.forEach((duty, dutyIndex) => {
      doc.setFontSize(9);
      doc.text(`Duty ${dutyIndex + 1}`, 14, currentYPosition);
      currentYPosition += 5; // Add some space between the duty header and table

      // Trip header and data
      const tripTable = [
        [
          "Date",
          "Type of Work",
          "Train No.",
          "Source",
          "Dest.",
          "Dep. Time",
