            day: "2-digit",
            month: "2-digit",
            year: "numeric",
          }),
          trip.typeOfWork,
          trip.trainNumber,
          trip.sourceStation,
          trip.destinationStation,
          trip.arrivalTime,
          trip.departureTime,
          trip.distance.toFixed(2).toString(),
          formatDutyHours(trip.dutyHours),
          trip.allowance.toString(),
          trip.remarks,
        ]),
      ];

      doc.autoTable({
        head: [tripTable[0]], // Use the first row as the header
        body: tripTable.slice(1), // Use the remaining rows as the body
        theme: "grid",
        styles: { fontSize: 8 },
        startY: currentYPosition, // Start the table at the current y position
      });

      currentYPosition = doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 2; // Update the current y position

      // DutyInfo
      const dutyInfoData = [
        [
          {
            content: "Sign On",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: duty.signIn
              ? new Date(duty.signIn).toLocaleString("en-GB", {
                  day: "2-digit",
                  month: "2-digit",
                  year: "numeric",
                  hour: "2-digit",
                  minute: "2-digit",
                })
              : "-",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: "Sign Off",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: duty.signOff
              ? new Date(duty.signOff).toLocaleString("en-GB", {
                  day: "2-digit",
                  month: "2-digit",
                  year: "numeric",
                  hour: "2-digit",
                  minute: "2-digit",
                })
              : "-",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: "KM earned:",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: `${duty.calculateTotalAllowance().toFixed(2)}`,
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: "Total Distance:",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: `${duty.calculateTotalDistance().toFixed(2)} km`,
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: "Duty Hours",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: formatHours(duty.dutyHours.toFixed(2)),
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: "Night Duty Hours :",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content: formatHours(duty.nightDutyHours.toFixed(2)),
            colSpan: 1,
          },
        ],
        [
          {
            content: "Progressive Duty Hours",
            colSpan: 2,
          },
          {
            content: formatHours(duty.progressiveDutyHours.toFixed(2)),
            colSpan: 2,
          },
          {
            content: "OSDA",
            colSpan: 2,
          },
          {
            content: duty.isOSDA ? "Yes" : "No",
            colSpan: 2,
          },
          {
            content: "RRA",
            colSpan: 2,
          },
          {
            content: duty.isRRA ? "Yes" : "No",
            colSpan: 2,
          },
        ],
      ];

      doc.autoTable({
        head: [],
        body: dutyInfoData,
        theme: "grid",
        styles: { fontSize: 8 },
        startY: currentYPosition, // Start the table at the current y position
      });

      currentYPosition = doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 10; // Update the current y position
    });

    // Add summary block data and overtime result

    currentYPosition = doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 10; // Update the current y position

    const overtimeResult = null;

    if (overtimeResult) {
      const overtimeResultData = [
        [
          { content: "Fortnight", colSpan: 1, styles: { fontStyle: "bold" } },
          { content: "Start Date", colSpan: 1, styles: { fontStyle: "bold" } },
          { content: "End Date", colSpan: 1, styles: { fontStyle: "bold" } },
          {
            content: "Overtime Hours",
            colSpan: 1,
            styles: { fontStyle: "bold" },
          },
          {
            content: "Overtime Minutes",
            colSpan: 1,
            styles: { fontStyle: "bold" },
          },
          {
            content: "Overtime Unit",
            colSpan: 1,
            styles: { fontStyle: "bold" },
          },
        ],
        [
          { content: "First Fortnight", colSpan: 1 },
          {
            content:
              overtimeResult.firstFortnight?.startDate.toDateString() || "-",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content:
              overtimeResult.firstFortnight?.endDate.toDateString() || "-",
            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content:
              overtimeResult.firstFortnight?.overtimeHours.toString() || "-",

            colSpan: 1,
          },
          {
            content:
              overtimeResult.secondFortnight?.overtimeHours.toString() || "-",

            colSpan: 1,

    // Generate the fortnights table
    const fortnightsData = [
      [
        { content: "Fortnight", colSpan: 1, styles: { fontStyle: "bold" } },
        { content: "Start Date", colSpan: 1, styles: { fontStyle: "bold" } },

    });

    currentYPosition = doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 10; // Update the current y position

    const summaryBlockData = [
      [
        { content: "Mileage:", colSpan: 1, styles: { fontStyle: "bold" } },
        { content: mileage ? `Rs. ${mileage.toFixed(2)}` : "-", colSpan: 1 },

          colSpan: 1,
        },
      ],

      [
        {
          content: "Leave Mileage Allowance:",
          colSpan: 1,
          styles: { fontStyle: "bold" },
        },
        {
          content: leaveMileageAllowance
            ? `Rs. ${leaveMileageAllowance.toFixed(2)}`
            : "-",
          colSpan: 1,
        },
      ],
      [
        {
          content: "Dearness Allowance:",
          colSpan: 1,
          styles: { fontStyle: "bold" },
        },
        {
          content: dearnessAllowance
            ? `Rs. ${dearnessAllowance.toFixed(2)}`
            : "-",
          colSpan: 1,
        },
      ],
      [
        {
          content: "RRA / OSDA:",
          colSpan: 1,
          styles: { fontStyle: "bold" },
        },
        {
          content: rraOsda ? `Rs. ${rraOsda.toFixed(2)}` : "-",
          colSpan: 1,
        },
      ],
      [
        {
          content: "Grand Total Allowance:",
          colSpan: 1,
          styles: { fontStyle: "bold" },
        },
        {
          content: grandTotalAllowance
            ? `Rs. ${grandTotalAllowance.toFixed(2)}`
            : "-",
          colSpan: 1,
        },
      ],
    ];

    doc.autoTable({
      head: [
        [
          {
            content: "Summary Block",
            colSpan: 2,
            styles: {
              fontStyle: "bold",
              fillColor: [200, 230, 245],
              textColor: [0, 0, 0],
            },
          },
        ],
      ],
      body: summaryBlockData,
      theme: "grid",
      styles: { fontSize: 8 },
      startY: currentYPosition, // Start the table at the current y position
    });

    doc.save("duty-report.pdf");
  };
  return (
    <View>
      <Button title="Generate PDF" onPress={generatePDF} />
    </View>
  );
};
export default PDFGenerator;



File: ./components/trip/Trip.tsx
----------------------------------------
class Trip {
  id: number;
  date: string;
  typeOfWork: string;
  trainNumber:string;
  departureTime: string;
  arrivalTime: string;
  timeDifference: number;
  sourceStation: string;
  destinationStation: string;
  distance: number;
  dutyHours:string;
  allowance: number;
  remarks: string;

  constructor(id: number) {
    this.id = id;
    this.date = "";
    this.typeOfWork = "";
    this.departureTime = "";
    this.arrivalTime = "";
    this.timeDifference = 0;
    this.sourceStation = "";
    this.destinationStation = "";
    this.distance = 0;
    this.allowance = 0;
    this.trainNumber="";
    this.remarks = "";
    this.dutyHours="";
  }
}

export default Trip;
export {};



File: ./components/trainNumber.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, TextInput, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';

interface TrainNumberInputProps {
  onChange: (trainNumber: string) => void;
  disabled: boolean;
}

const TrainNumberInput: React.FC<TrainNumberInputProps> = ({ onChange,disabled }) => {
  const [trainNumber, setTrainNumber] = useState('');

  const handleInputChange = (text: string) => {
    setTrainNumber(text);
    onChange(text); // Propagate the change to the parent component
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <TextInput
        style={styles.input}
        onChangeText={handleInputChange}
        value={trainNumber}
        placeholder="Enter train number"
        keyboardType="default" 
        disabled={disabled}
      />
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    // Container styles
  },
  input: {
    borderWidth:1,
    padding:5,
    height: 35,
    width: 150,
  }
});

export default TrainNumberInput;

import { useState } from 'react';
import Duty from '../duty/Duty';

const useDutyEndModal = (duty: Duty, updateDutyDetails: (updatedDuty: Duty) => void) => {
  const [dutyStartDate, setDutyStartDate] = useState<Date>(new Date());
  const [dutyStartTime, setDutyStartTime] = useState<Date>(new Date());
  const [dutyEndDate, setDutyEndDate] = useState<Date>(new Date());
  const [dutyEndTime, setDutyEndTime] = useState<Date>(new Date());
  const [isOSDAApplicable, setIsOSDAApplicable] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isRRAApplicable, setIsRRAApplicable] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isStartDatePickerVisible, setIsStartDatePickerVisible] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isStartTimePickerVisible, setIsStartTimePickerVisible] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isEndDatePickerVisible, setIsEndDatePickerVisible] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isEndTimePickerVisible, setIsEndTimePickerVisible] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const handleEndDuty = () => {
    const signInDateTime = new Date(dutyStartDate.getFullYear(), dutyStartDate.getMonth(), dutyStartDate.getDate(), dutyStartTime.getHours(), dutyStartTime.getMinutes());
    const signOffDateTime = new Date(dutyEndDate.getFullYear(), dutyEndDate.getMonth(), dutyEndDate.getDate(), dutyEndTime.getHours(), dutyEndTime.getMinutes());

    const updatedDuty = new Duty(duty.id, duty.trips);
    updatedDuty.setSignInAndSignOff(signInDateTime, signOffDateTime);
    updatedDuty.isOSDA = isOSDAApplicable;
    updatedDuty.isRRA = isRRAApplicable;

    updateDutyDetails(updatedDuty);
  };

  const toggleOSDAApplicable = () => setIsOSDAApplicable(!isOSDAApplicable);
  const toggleRRAApplicable = () => setIsRRAApplicable(!isRRAApplicable);

  const showStartDatePicker = () => setIsStartDatePickerVisible(true);
  const hideStartDatePicker = () => setIsStartDatePickerVisible(false);
  const handleStartDateConfirm = (date: Date) => {
    setDutyStartDate(date);
    setIsStartDatePickerVisible(false);
  };

  const showStartTimePicker = () => setIsStartTimePickerVisible(true);
  const hideStartTimePicker = () => setIsStartTimePickerVisible(false);
  const handleStartTimeConfirm = (time: Date) => {
    setDutyStartTime(time);
    setIsStartTimePickerVisible(false);
  };

  const showEndDatePicker = () => setIsEndDatePickerVisible(true);
  const hideEndDatePicker = () => setIsEndDatePickerVisible(false);
  const handleEndDateConfirm = (date: Date) => {
    setDutyEndDate(date);
    setIsEndDatePickerVisible(false);
  };

  const showEndTimePicker = () => setIsEndTimePickerVisible(true);
  const hideEndTimePicker = () => setIsEndTimePickerVisible(false);
  const handleEndTimeConfirm = (time: Date) => {
    setDutyEndTime(time);
    setIsEndTimePickerVisible(false);
  };

  const handleStartDateChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const date = new Date(event.target.value);
    if (!isNaN(date.getTime())) {
      setDutyStartDate(date);
    }
  };

  const handleStartTimeChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const [hours, minutes] = event.target.value.split(':');
    const date = new Date(dutyStartDate);
    date.setHours(parseInt(hours, 10));
    date.setMinutes(parseInt(minutes, 10));
    setDutyStartTime(date);
  };

  const handleEndDateChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const date = new Date(event.target.value);
    setDutyEndDate(date);
  };

  const handleEndTimeChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const [hours, minutes] = event.target.value.split(':');
    const date = new Date(dutyEndDate);
    date.setHours(parseInt(hours, 10));
    date.setMinutes(parseInt(minutes, 10));
    setDutyEndTime(date);
  };

  return {
    dutyStartDate,
    dutyStartTime,
    dutyEndDate,
    dutyEndTime,
    isOSDAApplicable,
    isRRAApplicable,
    isStartDatePickerVisible,
    isStartTimePickerVisible,
    isEndDatePickerVisible,
    isEndTimePickerVisible,
    handleEndDuty,
    toggleOSDAApplicable,
    toggleRRAApplicable,
    showStartDatePicker,
    hideStartDatePicker,
    handleStartDateConfirm,
    showStartTimePicker,
    hideStartTimePicker,
    handleStartTimeConfirm,
    showEndDatePicker,
    hideEndDatePicker,
    handleEndDateConfirm,
    showEndTimePicker,
    hideEndTimePicker,
    handleEndTimeConfirm,
    handleStartDateChange,
    handleStartTimeChange,
    handleEndDateChange,
    handleEndTimeChange,
  };
};

export default useDutyEndModal;


File: ./components/end-duty-modal/EndDutyModal.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { Platform, Text, View, Button, ScrollView, StyleSheet,TextInput, TouchableOpacity,Dimensions } from 'react-native';
import Duty from '../duty/Duty';
import DateTimePickerModal from 'react-native-modal-datetime-picker';
import { monthNames } from '../../utils/constants';

// Use Dimensions to get window size for responsive design
const { width } = Dimensions.get('window');

interface EndDutyModalProps {
  duty: Duty;
  updateDutyDetails: (updatedDuty: Duty) => void;
  closeModal: () => void;
  selectedMonth: keyof typeof monthNames;
}

interface EndDutyModalState {
  dutyStartDate: Date;
  dutyStartTime: Date;
  dutyEndDate: Date;
  dutyEndTime: Date;
  isOSDAApplicable: boolean;
  isRRAApplicable: boolean;
  isStartDatePickerVisible: boolean;
  isStartTimePickerVisible: boolean;
  isEndDatePickerVisible: boolean;
  isEndTimePickerVisible: boolean;
}

class EndDutyModal extends React.Component<EndDutyModalProps, EndDutyModalState> {
  constructor(props: EndDutyModalProps) {
    super(props);
    const { selectedMonth, duty } = props;
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    const monthNumber = monthNames[selectedMonth];
    const firstTripDate = duty.trips.length > 0 ? duty.trips[0].date : '';
    const defaultStartDate = firstTripDate ? new Date(firstTripDate) : new Date(currentYear, monthNumber - 1, 1);

    let defaultEndDate = new Date(defaultStartDate);
    let defaultEndTime = new Date(defaultStartDate);
  
    if (duty.trips.length > 0) {
      const lastTrip = duty.trips[duty.trips.length - 1];
      const lastTripDepartureTime = lastTrip.departureTime;
      const lastTripDate = lastTrip.date;
  
      if (lastTripDate) {
        defaultEndDate = new Date(lastTripDate);
      }
  
      if (lastTripDepartureTime) {
        const [hours, minutes] = lastTripDepartureTime.split(':');
        defaultEndTime.setHours(parseInt(hours, 10));
        defaultEndTime.setMinutes(parseInt(minutes, 10) + 30);
  
        // Adjust the defaultEndDate if the defaultEndTime crosses midnight
        if (defaultEndTime < defaultStartDate) {
          defaultEndDate.setDate(defaultEndDate.getDate() + 1);
        }
      }
    }  

    this.state = {
      dutyStartDate: defaultStartDate,
      dutyStartTime: defaultStartDate,
      dutyEndDate: defaultEndDate,
      dutyEndTime: defaultEndTime, 
      isOSDAApplicable: false,
      isRRAApplicable: false,
      isStartDatePickerVisible: false,
      isStartTimePickerVisible: false,
      isEndDatePickerVisible: false,
      isEndTimePickerVisible: false,
    };
  }
  handleEndDuty = () => {
    const { duty, updateDutyDetails } = this.props;
    const { dutyStartDate, dutyStartTime, dutyEndDate, dutyEndTime, isOSDAApplicable, isRRAApplicable } = this.state;
  
    const signInDateTime = new Date(dutyStartDate.getFullYear(), dutyStartDate.getMonth(), dutyStartDate.getDate(), dutyStartTime.getHours(), dutyStartTime.getMinutes());
    const signOffDateTime = new Date(dutyEndDate.getFullYear(), dutyEndDate.getMonth(), dutyEndDate.getDate(), dutyEndTime.getHours(), dutyEndTime.getMinutes());
  
    const timeDiffInMinutes = Math.round((signOffDateTime.getTime() - signInDateTime.getTime()) / 60000);
    const dutyHours = timeDiffInMinutes / 60; // Convert minutes to hours
  
    const updatedDuty = new Duty(duty.id, duty.trips);
    updatedDuty.setSignInAndSignOff(signInDateTime, signOffDateTime);
    updatedDuty.isOSDA = isOSDAApplicable;
    updatedDuty.isRRA = isRRAApplicable;
    updatedDuty.dutyHours = dutyHours;
    updatedDuty.hasEnded = true; // Set hasEnded to true
  
    updateDutyDetails(updatedDuty);
  };
  handleStartDateChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const date = new Date(event.target.value);
    if (!isNaN(date.getTime())) { // Check if the date is valid
      this.setState({ dutyStartDate: date });
    }
  };
  
  handleStartTimeChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const [hours, minutes] = event.target.value.split(':');
    const date = new Date(this.state.dutyStartDate);
    date.setHours(parseInt(hours, 10));
    date.setMinutes(parseInt(minutes, 10));
    this.setState({ dutyStartTime: date });
  };
  
  handleEndDateChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const date = new Date(event.target.value);
    this.setState({ dutyEndDate: date });
  };
  
  handleEndTimeChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const [hours, minutes] = event.target.value.split(':');
    const date = new Date(this.state.dutyEndDate);
    date.setHours(parseInt(hours, 10));
    date.setMinutes(parseInt(minutes, 10));
    this.setState({ dutyEndTime: date });
  };
  



  showStartDatePicker = () => {
    this.setState({ isStartDatePickerVisible: true });
  };

  hideStartDatePicker = () => {
    this.setState({ isStartDatePickerVisible: false });
  };

  handleStartDateConfirm = (date: Date) => {
    this.setState({ dutyStartDate: date, isStartDatePickerVisible: false });
  };

  showStartTimePicker = () => {
    this.setState({ isStartTimePickerVisible: true });
  };

  hideStartTimePicker = () => {
    this.setState({ isStartTimePickerVisible: false });
  };

  handleStartTimeConfirm = (time: Date) => {
    this.setState({ dutyStartTime: time, isStartTimePickerVisible: false });
  };

  showEndDatePicker = () => {
    this.setState({ isEndDatePickerVisible: true });
  };

  hideEndDatePicker = () => {
    this.setState({ isEndDatePickerVisible: false });
  };

  handleEndDateConfirm = (date: Date) => {
    this.setState({ dutyEndDate: date, isEndDatePickerVisible: false });
  };

  showEndTimePicker = () => {
    this.setState({ isEndTimePickerVisible: true });
  };

  hideEndTimePicker = () => {
    this.setState({ isEndTimePickerVisible: false });
  };

  handleEndTimeConfirm = (time: Date) => {
    this.setState({ dutyEndTime: time, isEndTimePickerVisible: false });
  };

  render() {
    const { closeModal } = this.props;
    const { dutyStartDate, dutyStartTime, dutyEndDate, dutyEndTime, isOSDAApplicable, isRRAApplicable, isStartDatePickerVisible, isStartTimePickerVisible, isEndDatePickerVisible, isEndTimePickerVisible } = this.state;    
    
      return (
        <View style={styles.container}>
          <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollViewContent}>
            <Text style={styles.heading}>End Duty</Text>
            {Platform.OS === 'web' ? (
              <>
                <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>Duty Start Date:</Text>
                  <input
                type="date"
                value={`${dutyStartDate.getFullYear()}-${String(dutyStartDate.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(dutyStartDate.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`}
                onChange={this.handleStartDateChange}
              />
                </View>
                <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>Duty Start Time:</Text>
                  <input
                type="time"
                value={`${String(dutyStartTime.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(dutyStartTime.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`}
                onChange={this.handleStartTimeChange}
              />

                </View>
                <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>Duty End Date:</Text>
                  <input
                type="date"
                value={`${dutyEndDate.getFullYear()}-${String(dutyEndDate.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(dutyEndDate.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`}
                onChange={this.handleEndDateChange}
              />
                </View>
                <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
                  <Text style={styles.label}>Duty End Time:</Text>
                  <input
                type="time"
                value={`${String(dutyEndTime.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(dutyEndTime.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`}
                onChange={this.handleEndTimeChange}
              />

                    </View>
                    </>
                    ) : (
                    <>
                    <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
                    <Text style={styles.label}>Duty Start Date:</Text>
                    <TouchableOpacity style={styles.inputButton} onPress={this.showStartDatePicker}>
                    <Text style={styles.inputText}>{dutyStartDate.toDateString()}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    <DateTimePickerModal
                                   isVisible={isStartDatePickerVisible}
                                   mode="date"
                                   onConfirm={this.handleStartDateConfirm}
                                   onCancel={this.hideStartDatePicker}
                                 />
                    </View>
                    <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Duty Start Time:</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.inputButton} onPress={this.showStartTimePicker}>
              <Text style={styles.inputText}>{dutyStartTime.toLocaleTimeString()}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <DateTimePickerModal
              isVisible={isStartTimePickerVisible}
              mode="time"
              onConfirm={this.handleStartTimeConfirm}
              onCancel={this.hideStartTimePicker}
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Duty End Date:</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.inputButton} onPress={this.showEndDatePicker}>
              <Text style={styles.inputText}>{dutyEndDate.toDateString()}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <DateTimePickerModal
              isVisible={isEndDatePickerVisible}
              mode="date"
              onConfirm={this.handleEndDateConfirm}
              onCancel={this.hideEndDatePicker}
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Duty End Time:</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.inputButton} onPress={this.showEndTimePicker}>
              <Text style={styles.inputText}>{dutyEndTime.toLocaleTimeString()}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <DateTimePickerModal
              isVisible={isEndTimePickerVisible}
              mode="time"
              onConfirm={this.handleEndTimeConfirm}
              onCancel={this.hideEndTimePicker}
            />
          </View>
        </>
      )}

      <View style={styles.toggleContainer}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>Is OSDA Applicable:</Text>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.toggleButton, isOSDAApplicable && styles.toggleButtonActive]}
          onPress={() => this.setState({ isOSDAApplicable: !isOSDAApplicable })}
        >
          <Text style={styles.toggleButtonText}>{isOSDAApplicable ? 'Yes' : 'No'}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.toggleContainer}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>Is RRA Applicable:</Text>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.toggleButton, isRRAApplicable && styles.toggleButtonActive]}
          onPress={() => this.setState({ isRRAApplicable: !isRRAApplicable })}
        >
          <Text style={styles.toggleButtonText}>{isRRAApplicable ? 'Yes' : 'No'}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.buttonContainer}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.button} onPress={this.handleEndDuty}>
          <Text style={styles.buttonText}>End Duty</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity style={[styles.button, styles.cancelButton]} onPress={closeModal}>
          <Text style={styles.buttonText}>Cancel</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  </View>
);}
}

File: ./components/arrivalDepartureTImePIcker/ArrivalDepartureTimePicker.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Platform,
  TouchableOpacity,
} from "react-native";
import TimePicker from "../time-picker/TimePicker";
import DateTimePicker from "@react-native-community/datetimepicker";

interface ArrivalDepartureTimePickerProps {
  onTimesChange: (
    arrival: string,
    departure: string,
    dutyHours: string
  ) => void;
  disabled: boolean;
}

const ArrivalDepartureTimePicker: React.FC<ArrivalDepartureTimePickerProps> = ({
  onTimesChange,
  disabled,
}) => {
  const [arrivalTime, setArrivalTime] = useState("");
  const [departureTime, setDepartureTime] = useState("");
  const [showArrivalPicker, setShowArrivalPicker] = useState(false);
  const [showDeparturePicker, setShowDeparturePicker] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (arrivalTime && departureTime) {
      const timeDiff = calculateTimeDifference(arrivalTime, departureTime);
      onTimesChange(arrivalTime, departureTime, timeDiff);
    }
  }, [arrivalTime, departureTime]);

  const calculateTimeDifference = (arrival: string, departure: string) => {
    const arrivalDate = new Date(`2021/01/01 ${arrival}`);
    const departureDate = new Date(`2021/01/01 ${departure}`);

    // Check if arrival time is less than departure time
    if (arrivalDate.getTime() > departureDate.getTime()) {
      // Increment the departure date by one day
      departureDate.setDate(departureDate.getDate() + 1);
    }

    const timeDiff = getTimeDifference(arrivalDate, departureDate);
    const hours = Math.floor(timeDiff / 60);
    const minutes = timeDiff % 60;
    return `${hours}h ${minutes}m`;
  };

  const getTimeDifference = (
    arrivalTime: Date,
    departureTime: Date
  ): number => {
    if (!arrivalTime || !departureTime) {
      return 0;
    }

    // Check if arrival and departure times are equal
    if (arrivalTime.getTime() === departureTime.getTime()) {
      // Special case: both times are at midnight
      if (arrivalTime.getHours() === 0 && arrivalTime.getMinutes() === 0) {
        return 24 * 60;
      }
      return 0;
    }

    if (
      arrivalTime.getHours() === 0 &&
      arrivalTime.getMinutes() === 0 &&
      departureTime.getHours() === 0 &&
      departureTime.getMinutes() === 0
    ) {
      return 24 * 60;
    }

    const timeDiff = Math.round(
      (departureTime.getTime() - arrivalTime.getTime()) / 60000
    );

    // Return 0 if the time difference is negative
    return timeDiff >= 0 ? timeDiff : 0;
  };

  const handleArrivalTimeChange = (
    event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>
  ) => {
    setArrivalTime(event.target.value);
  };

  const handleDepartureTimeChange = (
    event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>
  ) => {
    setDepartureTime(event.target.value);
  };

  const openArrivalPicker = () => {
    setShowArrivalPicker(true);
  };

  const openDeparturePicker = () => {
    setShowDeparturePicker(true);
  };

  const handleTimeChange =
    (isArrival: boolean) => (event: any, selectedTime?: Date) => {
      if (event.type === "set" && selectedTime) {
        const hour = String(selectedTime.getHours()).padStart(2, "0");
        const minute = String(selectedTime.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0");
        const timeString = `${hour}:${minute}`;

        if (isArrival) {
          setArrivalTime(timeString);
          setShowArrivalPicker(false);
        } else {
          setDepartureTime(timeString);
          setShowDeparturePicker(false);
        }
      } else {
        setShowArrivalPicker(false);
        setShowDeparturePicker(false);
      }
    };

  const renderTimePicker = (isArrival: boolean) => {
    const time = isArrival ? arrivalTime : departureTime;
    const setShowPicker = isArrival
      ? setShowArrivalPicker
      : setShowDeparturePicker;
    const showPicker = isArrival ? showArrivalPicker : showDeparturePicker;

    return (
      <View style={styles.timePickerContainer}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>{isArrival ? "Dep:" : "Arr:"}</Text>
        {Platform.OS === "web" ? (
          <input
            type="time"
            value={time}
            onChange={(e) =>
              isArrival
                ? setArrivalTime(e.target.value)
                : setDepartureTime(e.target.value)
            }
            disabled={disabled}
            style={styles.timeInput}
          />
        ) : (
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setShowPicker(true)}
            disabled={disabled}
          >
            <View style={styles.timeContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.timeText}>{time || "--:--"}</Text>
            </View>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
        {Platform.OS !== "web" && showPicker && (
          <DateTimePicker
            value={new Date()}
            mode="time"
            is24Hour={true}
            display="default"
            onChange={handleTimeChange(isArrival)}
          />
        )}
      </View>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {renderTimePicker(true)}
      {renderTimePicker(false)}
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    width: "100%",
  },
  timePickerContainer: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    marginBottom: 4,
  },
  label: {
    fontSize: 10,
    marginRight: 4,
  },
  timeContainer: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "gray",
    borderRadius: 4,
    padding: 2,
  },
  timeText: {
    fontSize: 12,
  },
  timeInput: {
    width: "70%",
    height: 24,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "gray",
    borderRadius: 4,
    padding: 2,
    fontSize: 12,
  },
});

export default ArrivalDepartureTimePicker;

// CalculationPage.tsx
import React from "react";
import { View, Text, ScrollView } from "react-native";
import MainTable from "../main-table/MainTable";
import { calculationPageStyles as styles } from "./calculationPageStyles";
import { RouteProp } from '@react-navigation/native';
import { NavigationProp } from '@react-navigation/native';

type RootStackParamList = {
  CalculationPage: {
    designation: string;
    payBand: number;
    level: number;
    rateOfPay: number;
    selectedMonth: string;
  };
};

type CalculationPageRouteProp = RouteProp<RootStackParamList, 'CalculationPage'>;

interface CalculationPageProps {
  route: CalculationPageRouteProp;
  navigation: NavigationProp<RootStackParamList>;
}

const CalculationPage: React.FC<CalculationPageProps> = ({ route, navigation }) => {
  const { designation, payBand, level, rateOfPay, selectedMonth } = route.params;

  return (
    <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContainer}>
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.background}>
          <View style={styles.row}>
            <Text style={styles.text}>Month: {selectedMonth}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.row}>
            <Text style={styles.text}>Designation: {designation}</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.row}>
            <Text style={styles.text}>Level: {level}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.text}>Pay Band: {payBand}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.text}>Rate of Pay: {rateOfPay}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
        <MainTable
          navigation={navigation}
          rateOfPay={rateOfPay}
          designation={designation}
          payBand={payBand}
          level={level}
          selectedMonth={selectedMonth}
        />
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
};

File: ./components/HomeScreen.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React, { Component } from 'react';
import { View, ScrollView, Text, StyleSheet, Platform, TouchableOpacity } from 'react-native';
import { Picker } from '@react-native-picker/picker';
import InterdependentPickers from './InterdependentPicker';
import { styles } from '../styles_component/styles_homescreen';
import { designations, months } from '../utils/constants';

interface EmployeeInfoProps {
  navigation: any; // You can define a more specific type based on your navigation structure
}

interface EmployeeInfoState {
  designation: string;
  payBand: React.SetStateAction<string>;
  level: React.SetStateAction<string>;
  rateOfPay: React.SetStateAction<string>;
  selectedMonth: string;
  showWarning: boolean;
}

class EmployeeInfo extends Component<EmployeeInfoProps, EmployeeInfoState> {
  constructor(props: EmployeeInfoProps) {
    super(props);
    this.state = {
      designation: '',
      payBand: '',
      level: '',
      rateOfPay: '',
      selectedMonth: '',
      showWarning: false,
    };
  }

  onContinuePress = () => {
    const { designation, payBand, level, rateOfPay, selectedMonth } = this.state;
    if (!designation || !payBand || !level || !rateOfPay || !selectedMonth) {
      this.setState({ showWarning: true });
    } else {
      const selectedDesignation = designations.find((item) => item.label === designation);
  const designationValue = selectedDesignation ? selectedDesignation.value : '';
      this.setState({ showWarning: false });
      this.props.navigation.navigate('CalculationPage', {
        designation: designationValue, 
        payBand: payBand,
        level: level,
        rateOfPay: rateOfPay,
        selectedMonth: selectedMonth,
      });
    }
    
  };

  render() {
    const { designation, payBand, level, rateOfPay, selectedMonth, showWarning } = this.state;
    return (
      <ScrollView style={{flex: 1}}>
        <View style={[styles.homepage, styles.homepageFlexBox]}>
          <View style={[styles.allowanceCalculator, styles.homepageFlexBox]}>
            <Text style={styles.allowanceCalculator1}>ALLOWANCE</Text>
            <Text style={styles.allowanceCalculator1}>CALCULATOR</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.homepageChild}>
            <View
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                ...(Platform.OS === 'ios' && { marginBottom: 210 }),
              }}
            >
              <Picker
                selectedValue={designation}
                onValueChange={(itemValue) => this.setState({ designation: itemValue })}
                style={{ height: 40 }}
              >
                {designations.map((item, index) => (
                  <Picker.Item key={index} label={item.label} value={item.value} />
                ))}
              </Picker>
            </View>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.homepageItem, styles.homepageBg]}>
            <View
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                ...(Platform.OS === 'ios' && { marginBottom: 210 }),
              }}
            >
              <Picker
                style={{ height: 40 }}
                selectedValue={selectedMonth}
                onValueChange={(itemValue) => this.setState({ selectedMonth: itemValue })}
              >
                {months.map((item, index) => (
                  <Picker.Item key={index} label={item.label} value={item.value} />
                ))}
              </Picker>
            </View>
          </View>
          <View style={[styles.homepageInner, styles.homepageBg]}>
            <InterdependentPickers
              payBand={payBand}
              setPayBand={(value) => this.setState({ payBand: value })}
              level={level}
              setLevel={(value) => this.setState({ level: value })}
              rateOfPay={rateOfPay}
              setRateOfPay={(value) => this.setState({ rateOfPay: value })}
            />
          </View>
          {showWarning && (
            <Text style={{ color: 'red', textAlign: 'center', marginVertical: 10 }}>
             Enter Employee Information
            </Text>
          )}
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.7}
            style={[
              styles.rectanglePressable,
              styles.homepageBg,
              (!designation || !payBand || !level || !rateOfPay || !selectedMonth) && { opacity: 0.5 },
            ]}
            onPress={this.onContinuePress}
          >
            <Text style={styles.proceedText}>Proceed</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    );
  }
}


export default EmployeeInfo;

import React from 'react';
import { Modal as RNModal, View, TouchableOpacity, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';

interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

const Modal: React.FC<ModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, children }) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <RNModal animationType="fade" transparent={true} visible={isOpen}>
      <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
        <View style={styles.modalContent}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.closeButton} onPress={onClose}>
            <Text style={styles.closeButtonText}>Close</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          {children}
        </View>
      </View>
    </RNModal>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  modalOverlay: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  modalContent: {
    backgroundColor: '#fff',
    padding: 20,
    borderRadius: 8,
  },
  closeButton: {
    alignSelf: 'flex-end',
    marginBottom: 10,
  },
  closeButtonText: {
    color: '#007bff',
    fontWeight: 'bold',
  },
});

export default Modal;


File: ./components/duty/DutyRow.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Platform,
  View,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
} from "react-native";
import Duty from "./Duty";
import Trip from "../trip/Trip";
import TypeofWorkPickerComponent from "../type-of-work/typeofwork";
import PickerComponentStation from "../station-picker/stationPicker";
import TrainNumberInput from "../trainNumber";
import ArrivalDepartureTimePicker from "../arrivalDepartureTImePIcker/ArrivalDepartureTimePicker";
import DutyInfo from "./DutyInfo";
import calculateAllowanceAndRemarks from "../allowancecalculation/calculateAllowanceAndRemarks";
import DatePickerComponent from "../DatePicker/DatePickerComponent";
import WebDatePicker from "../DatePicker/WebDatePicker";
import { monthNames, disabledTypes } from "../../utils/constants";
import styles from "../../styles_component/styles_dutyRow";

const debounce = (func, delay) => {
  let timeoutId;
  return (...args) => {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    timeoutId = setTimeout(() => func(...args), delay);
  };
};

interface DutyRowProps {
  duty: Duty;
  addTrip: (dutyId: number) => void;
  handleEndDuty: (duty: Duty) => void;
  updateTripData: (
    dutyId: number,
    tripId: number,
    field: keyof Trip,
    value: any
  ) => void;
  deleteTrip: (dutyId: number, tripId: number) => void;
  deleteDuty: (dutyId: number) => void;
  setTotalLeaves: (newTotalLeaves: number) => void;
  selectedMonth: keyof typeof monthNames; // Update the selectedMonth prop type
}

const DutyRow: React.FC<DutyRowProps> = ({
  duty,
  addTrip,
  handleEndDuty,
  updateTripData,
  deleteTrip,
  deleteDuty,
  setTotalLeaves,
  selectedMonth, // Receive the selectedMonth prop
}) => {
  const [hasEnded, setHasEnded] = useState(duty.hasEnded);

  const handleTripDataChange = (
    tripId: number,
    field: keyof Trip,
    value: any
  ) => {
    const tripIndex = duty.trips.findIndex((trip) => trip.id === tripId);
    const trip = duty.trips[tripIndex];

    const shouldDisableInputFields = (typeOfWork: string) => {
      return disabledTypes.includes(typeOfWork);
    };

    // Update the dutyHours for leave types before updating the trip data
    if (field === "typeOfWork") {
      duty.updateDutyHoursForLeaveTypes(value);

      // Check if the new type is a leave type and the previous type was not
      if (
        shouldDisableInputFields(value) &&
        !shouldDisableInputFields(trip.typeOfWork)
      ) {
        // Reset all values except date
        const updatedTrip = {
          ...trip,
          [field]: value,
          sourceStation: "",
          destinationStation: "",
          distance: 0,
          trainNumber: "",
          arrivalTime: "",
          departureTime: "",
          dutyHours: "",
          allowance: 0,
          remarks: "",
        };
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, field, value);
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "sourceStation", "");
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "destinationStation", "");
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "distance", 0);
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "trainNumber", "");
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "arrivalTime", "");
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "departureTime", "");
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "dutyHours", "");
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "allowance", 0);
        updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "remarks", "");

        // Set duty.hasEnded to true if the new type is a leave type
        duty.hasEnded = true;

        return;
      } else if (shouldDisableInputFields(value)) {
        // Set duty.hasEnded to true if the new type is a leave type
        duty.hasEnded = true;
      } else {
        // Reset duty.hasEnded to false if the new type is not a leave type
        duty.hasEnded = false;
      }
    }

    // Update the trip data
    const updatedTrip = { ...trip, [field]: value };
    updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, field, value);

    // Recalculate allowance and remarks whenever any field that affects the calculation is changed
    const dutyHoursInMinutes = calculateDutyHoursFromTimes(
      updatedTrip.arrivalTime,
      updatedTrip.departureTime
    );

    const { allowance, remarks } = calculateAllowanceAndRemarks(
      updatedTrip.distance,
      updatedTrip.typeOfWork,
      dutyHoursInMinutes
    );

    updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "allowance", allowance);
    updateTripData(duty.id, tripId, "remarks", remarks);
  };

  const handleTimesChange = useCallback(
    debounce((tripId, arrivalTime, departureTime, dutyHours) => {
      handleTripDataChange(tripId, "arrivalTime", arrivalTime);
      handleTripDataChange(tripId, "departureTime", departureTime);
      handleTripDataChange(tripId, "dutyHours", dutyHours);
    }, 300),
    [handleTripDataChange]
  );

  const calculateDutyHoursFromTimes = (
    arrivalTime: string,
    departureTime: string
  ): number => {
    if (!arrivalTime || !departureTime) {
      return 0;
    }

    const arrival = new Date(`1970-01-01T${arrivalTime}`);
    const departure = new Date(`1970-01-01T${departureTime}`);

    // Check if arrival time is less than departure time
    if (arrival.getTime() > departure.getTime()) {
      // Increment the arrival date by one day
      departure.setDate(departure.getDate() + 1);
    }

    const dutyHoursInMinutes =
      (departure.getTime() - arrival.getTime()) / (1000 * 60);

    return dutyHoursInMinutes;
  };

  const handleStationChange = (
    dutyId: number,
    tripId: number,
    sourceStation: string,
    destinationStation: string,
    distance: number
  ) => {
    updateTripData(dutyId, tripId, "sourceStation", sourceStation);
    updateTripData(dutyId, tripId, "destinationStation", destinationStation);
    updateTripData(dutyId, tripId, "distance", distance);

    // Recalculate the allowance based on the new distance
    duty.trips.forEach((trip) => {
      if (trip.id === tripId) {
        const dutyHoursAsNumber = parseFloat(trip.dutyHours) || 0;

        const { allowance, remarks } = calculateAllowanceAndRemarks(
          distance,
          trip.typeOfWork,
          dutyHoursAsNumber * 60
        );

        updateTripData(dutyId, tripId, "allowance", allowance);
        updateTripData(dutyId, tripId, "remarks", remarks);
      }
    });
  };

  const handleTrainNumberChange = (
    trainNumber: string,
    dutyId: number,
    tripId: number
  ) => {
    updateTripData(dutyId, tripId, "trainNumber", trainNumber);
  };

  const handleEditDuty = () => {
    setHasEnded(false);
    duty.hasEnded = false; // Directly modify the duty object
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    duty.trips.forEach((trip) => {
      const dutyHoursInMinutes = calculateDutyHoursFromTimes(
        trip.arrivalTime,
        trip.departureTime
      );

      const { allowance, remarks } = calculateAllowanceAndRemarks(
        trip.distance,
        trip.typeOfWork,
        dutyHoursInMinutes
      );

      updateTripData(duty.id, trip.id, "allowance", allowance);
      updateTripData(duty.id, trip.id, "remarks", remarks);
    });
  }, [
    duty.trips,
    updateTripData,
    duty.id,
    calculateDutyHoursFromTimes,
    calculateAllowanceAndRemarks,
  ]);

  const shouldDisableInputFields = (typeOfWork: string) => {
    return disabledTypes.includes(typeOfWork);
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.headerContainer}>
        <Text style={styles.serialNumber}>{duty.id}</Text>
      </View>
      <ScrollView horizontal={false} style={styles.tripsContainer}>
        {duty.trips.map((trip, index) => (
          <View key={trip.id} style={styles.tripContainer}>
            <View style={styles.dateContainer}>
              {Platform.OS === "web" ? (
                <WebDatePicker
                  value={
                    trip.date ||
                    `${new Date().getFullYear()}-${String(
                      monthNames[selectedMonth]
                    ).padStart(2, "0")}-01`
                  }
                  onChange={(date) =>
                    handleTripDataChange(trip.id, "date", date)
                  }
                  min={`2023-${String(monthNames[selectedMonth]).padStart(
                    2,
                    "0"
                  )}-01`}
                />
              ) : (
                <DatePickerComponent
                  onDateSelected={(date) =>
                    handleTripDataChange(trip.id, "date", date)
                  }
                  defaultDate={`${new Date().getFullYear()}-${
                    monthNames[selectedMonth]
                  }-01`}
                  minimumDate={
                    new Date(
                      `2023-${String(monthNames[selectedMonth]).padStart(
                        2,
                        "0"
                      )}-01`
                    )
                  }
                />
              )}
            </View>
            <View style={styles.dutyTypeContainer}>
              <TypeofWorkPickerComponent
                TypeofWork={(newValue) =>
                  handleTripDataChange(trip.id, "typeOfWork", newValue)
                }
              />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.trainNoContainer}>
              <TrainNumberInput
                onChange={(trainNumber) =>
                  handleTrainNumberChange(trainNumber, duty.id, trip.id)
                }
                disabled={shouldDisableInputFields(trip.typeOfWork)}
                maxLength={7}
              />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.stationsContainer}>
              <PickerComponentStation
                onStationChange={handleStationChange}
                dutyId={duty.id}
                tripId={trip.id}
                disabled={shouldDisableInputFields(trip.typeOfWork)}
                maxLength={5}
                duty={duty}
              />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.timePickerContainer}>
              <ArrivalDepartureTimePicker
                onTimesChange={(arrivalTime, departureTime, dutyHours) =>
                  handleTimesChange(
                    trip.id,
                    arrivalTime,
                    departureTime,
                    dutyHours
                  )
                }
                disabled={shouldDisableInputFields(trip.typeOfWork)}
              />
            </View>
            <View style={styles.durationContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.durationText}>{trip.dutyHours}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.durationLabel}>duration</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.allowanceContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.allowanceText}>
                {trip.allowance === 1 ? 0 : trip.allowance.toFixed(2)}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.allowanceLabel}>km earned</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.remarksContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.remarksText}>{trip.remarks}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.remarksLabel}>remarks</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.actionsContainer}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => deleteTrip(duty.id, trip.id)}
                disabled={duty.trips.length === 1}
                style={styles.deleteButton}
              >
                <Text style={styles.deleteButtonText}>Delete</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
      <View style={styles.buttonsContainer}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.button, styles.addTripButton]}
          onPress={() => addTrip(duty.id)}
          disabled={
            duty.hasEnded || shouldDisableInputFields(duty.trips[0].typeOfWork)
          }
        >
          <Text style={styles.buttonText}>Add Trip</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.button, styles.endDutyButton]}
          onPress={() => handleEndDuty(duty)}
          disabled={
            duty.hasEnded || shouldDisableInputFields(duty.trips[0].typeOfWork)
          }
        >
          <Text style={styles.buttonText}>End Duty</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.button, styles.deleteDutyButton]}
          onPress={() => deleteDuty(duty.id)}
        >
          <Text style={styles.buttonText}>Delete Duty</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.button, styles.editDutyButton]}
          onPress={handleEditDuty}
        >
          <Text style={styles.buttonText}>Edit Duty</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
      {duty.hasEnded && <DutyInfo duty={duty} />}
    </View>
  );
};

export default DutyRow;



File: ./components/duty/DutyInfo.tsx
----------------------------------------
import React from "react";
import Duty from "./Duty";
import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from "react-native";

interface DutyInfoProps {
  duty: Duty;
}

const DutyInfo: React.FC<DutyInfoProps> = ({ duty }) => {
  const totalDistance = 0 || duty.calculateTotalDistance();
 
  const formatHoursToHHMM = (hours: number) => {
    const wholeHours = Math.floor(hours);
    const decimalHours = hours - wholeHours;
    const minutes = Math.round(decimalHours * 60);
    return `${wholeHours.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${minutes.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.main}>
      <View style={styles.dutyInfoContainer}>
        <View style={styles.dutyContent}>
          <View style={styles.infoRow}>
            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Sign In</Text>
              <Text style={styles.value}>
              {duty.signIn
                  ? duty.signIn.toLocaleString('en-NZ', {
                      day: '2-digit',
                      month: '2-digit',
                      year: 'numeric',
                      hour: '2-digit',
                      minute: '2-digit',
                      hour12: false,
                    })
                  : '-'}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Sign Off</Text>

              <Text style={styles.value}>
              {duty.signOff
                  ? duty.signOff.toLocaleString('en-NZ', {
                      day: '2-digit',
                      month: '2-digit',
                      year: 'numeric',
                      hour: '2-digit',
                      minute: '2-digit',
                      hour12: false,
                    })
                  : '-'}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Total Km Earned:</Text>
              <Text style={styles.value}>
                {duty.calculateTotalAllowance().toFixed(2)}km
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Total Distance:</Text>
              <Text style={styles.value}>{totalDistance.toFixed(2)} km</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Duty Hours</Text>
              <Text style={styles.value}>
                {formatHoursToHHMM(duty.dutyHours)}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Night Duty Hours :</Text>
              <Text style={styles.value}>
                {formatHoursToHHMM(duty.nightDutyHours)}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.infoRow}>
            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Progressive Duty Hours</Text>
              <Text style={styles.value}>
                {formatHoursToHHMM(duty.progressiveDutyHours)}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.infoRow}>
            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>Progressive Night Duty Hours:</Text>
        <Text style={styles.value}>  {formatHoursToHHMM(duty.progressiveNightDutyHours)}</Text>
      </View>
      </View>
            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>OSDA</Text>
              <Text style={styles.value}>{duty.isOSDA ? "Yes" : "No"}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.infoBlock}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>RRA</Text>
              <Text style={styles.value}>{duty.isRRA ? "Yes" : "No"}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  main: {
    display: "flex",
    alignItems: "flex-start",
    justifyContent: "center",
    width: "100%",
  },
  dutyInfoContainer: {
    backgroundColor: "#f0f0f0",
    borderRadius: 8,
    margin: 10,
    padding: 8,
    width: "100%",
    maxWidth: "none",
  },
  dutyContent: {
    display: "flex",
    flexDirection: "column",
    alignItems: "flex-start",
    justifyContent: "flex-start",
  },
  infoRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    flexWrap: "wrap",
    justifyContent: "flex-start",
  },
  infoBlock: {
    flexShrink: 0,
    marginRight: 24,
    minWidth: 160,
  },
  label: {
    fontSize: 14,
    color: "#666",
    marginBottom: 4,
  },
  value: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontWeight: "bold",
    color: "#333",
  },
});

export default DutyInfo;



File: ./components/duty/Duty.tsx
----------------------------------------
import Trip from "../trip/Trip";
import { leaveTypes } from "../../utils/constants";

class Duty {
  id: number;
  trips: Trip[];
  signIn: Date | null;
  signOff: Date | null;
  dutyHours: number;
  nightDutyHours: number;
  isOSDA: boolean;
  isRRA: boolean;
  dutyAllowance: number;
  dutyDistance: number;
  progressiveDutyHours:number;
  progressiveNightDutyHours: number;
  hasEnded: boolean; // Add this line
  isLeave: boolean;

  constructor(id: number, trips: Trip[] = []) {
    this.id = id;
    this.trips = trips;
    this.signIn = null;
    this.signOff = null;
    this.dutyHours = 0;
    this.nightDutyHours = 0;
    this.isOSDA = false;
    this.isRRA = false;
    this.dutyAllowance = 0;
    this.dutyDistance = 0;
    this.progressiveDutyHours=0;
    this.progressiveNightDutyHours=0;
    this.hasEnded = false; // Initialize hasEnded
    this.isLeave = false;
  }

  setSignInAndSignOff(signIn: Date, signOff: Date) {
    this.signIn = signIn;
    this.signOff = signOff;
    this.calculateDutyHoursAndNightDutyHours();
  }

  private calculateDutyHoursAndNightDutyHours() {
    if (this.signIn && this.signOff) {
      this.dutyHours = this.calculateDutyHours();
      this.nightDutyHours = this.calculateNightDutyHours();
    // this.progressiveNightDutyHours += this.nightDutyHours;
    }
  }

  calculateTotalAllowance() {
    let totalAllowanceforDuty = 0;
  
    // Calculate the total allowance for non-leave trips
    const nonLeaveTripAllowances = this.trips
      .filter(trip => !["Paid leave", "Sick leave", "Casual leave"].includes(trip.typeOfWork))
      .map(trip => trip.allowance);
  
    totalAllowanceforDuty = nonLeaveTripAllowances.reduce((total, allowance) => total + allowance, 0);
  
    // Apply the minimum allowance of 120 only for non-leave trips
    if (totalAllowanceforDuty > 0 && totalAllowanceforDuty <= 120) {
      totalAllowanceforDuty = 120;
    }
  
    this.dutyAllowance = totalAllowanceforDuty;
    return totalAllowanceforDuty;
  }
  calculateTotalDistance() {
    const totalDistance = this.trips.reduce((acc, trip) => acc + (trip.distance || 0), 0);
    return totalDistance;
  }

  // calculateprogressiveDutyHours(dutyIndex: number, totalDuties: Duty[]): number {
  //   if (dutyIndex === 0) {
  //     // For the first duty, the progressiveDutyHours is the same as dutyHours
  //     this.progressiveDutyHours = this.dutyHours;
  //   } else {
  //     // Calculate the progressive duty hours based on the previous duty's progressiveDutyHours
  //     const previousDuty = totalDuties[dutyIndex - 1];
  //     this.progressiveDutyHours = previousDuty.progressiveDutyHours + this.dutyHours;
  //   }
  
  //   return this.progressiveDutyHours;
  // }

  calculateProgressiveNightDutyHours(dutyIndex: number, totalDuties: Duty[]): number {
    if (dutyIndex === 0) {
      // For the first duty, the progressiveNightDutyHours is the same as nightDutyHours
      this.progressiveNightDutyHours = this.nightDutyHours;
    } else {
      // Calculate the progressive night duty hours based on the previous duty's progressiveNightDutyHours
      const previousDuty = totalDuties[dutyIndex - 1];
      this.progressiveNightDutyHours = previousDuty.progressiveNightDutyHours + this.nightDutyHours;
    }

    return this.progressiveNightDutyHours;
}

updateDutyHoursForLeaveTypes(typeOfWork: string) {
  const endedTypes = ["Periodic rest", "Waiting for duty"];

  if (leaveTypes.includes(typeOfWork)) {
    this.dutyHours = 7.41667; // 7 hours and 25 minutes
    this.dutyAllowance = 0;
    this.dutyDistance = 0;
    this.isLeave = true;
  } else if (endedTypes.includes(typeOfWork)) {
    this.dutyHours = 0; // Set dutyHours to 0 for "Periodic Rest" and "Waiting for duty"
    this.dutyAllowance = 0;
    this.dutyDistance = 0;
  } else {
    this.dutyHours = this.calculateDutyHours();
    this.dutyAllowance = this.calculateTotalAllowance();
    this.dutyDistance = this.calculateTotalDistance();
  }
}
  updateTrip(tripId: number, updatedTrip: Trip) {
    const tripIndex = this.trips.findIndex((trip) => trip.id === tripId);
    if (tripIndex !== -1) {
      this.trips[tripIndex] = updatedTrip;
    }
  }

  private calculateDutyHours(): number {
    if (this.signIn && this.signOff) {
      const timeDiff = Math.abs(this.signOff.getTime() - this.signIn.getTime());
      const totalMinutes = Math.floor(timeDiff / (1000 * 60));
  
      if (totalMinutes < 60) {
        return totalMinutes / 60;
      } else {
        const hours = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60);
        const minutes = totalMinutes % 60;
        const fractionalHours = minutes / 60;
        return hours + fractionalHours;
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }
  
  private calculateNightDutyHours(): number {
    if (this.signIn && this.signOff) {
      const NIGHT_START_HOUR = 22; // 10 PM
      const NIGHT_END_HOUR = 6; // 6 AM
      let nightDutyMinutes = 0;

      // Start counting from signIn and stop at signOff
      let current = new Date(this.signIn);

      while (current < this.signOff) {
        let hour = current.getHours();
        if (hour >= NIGHT_START_HOUR || hour < NIGHT_END_HOUR) {
          nightDutyMinutes++; // Count each minute during the night hours
        }
        current.setMinutes(current.getMinutes() + 1); // Move to next minute
      }

      // Convert total minutes to hours in decimal
      let nightDutyHours = nightDutyMinutes / 60;

      return nightDutyHours;
    }

    return 0;
  }
}

export default Duty;

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, StyleSheet, ImageBackground, Dimensions, Image, Platform, ActivityIndicator } from 'react-native';
import backgroundImage from '../assets/bgimage6.png';
import googleIcon from '../assets/googleIcon.png';
import { useNavigation } from '@react-navigation/native';
import { auth } from '../firebase/firebaseConfig';
import { handleGoogleSignIn } from './handleGoogleSignIn';

const { width } = Dimensions.get('window');

const SignInScreen = () => {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const navigation = useNavigation();

  const handleSignIn = async () => {
    if (!email || !password) {
      setError('Please enter email and password');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email, password);
      navigation.navigate('Home');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const handleForgotPassword = () => {
    // Handle forgot password logic
  };

  function signInWithGoogle() {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      const { signInWithGoogleWeb } = require('./webGoogleSignIn');
      signInWithGoogleWeb(auth, navigation);
    } else {
      const { handleGoogleSignIn } = require('./handleGoogleSignIn');
      handleGoogleSignIn(auth, navigation);
    }
  }

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <ImageBackground source={backgroundImage} style={styles.backgroundImage}>
        <View style={styles.imageOverlay}>
          <View style={styles.titleContainer}>
            <View style={styles.titleBackground}>
              <Text style={styles.title}>Railway Allowance Calculator</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
          <View style={styles.contentContainer}>
            <View style={styles.formContainer}>
              <Text style={styles.description}>
                The Railway Allowance Calculator is designed for Indian Railways Employees, specifically operational employees, to calculate their allowance. The application can calculate the allowance for a month for multiple employee types, grades, and pay bands. It also generates a PDF report for easy record-keeping. The application is available on Android and iOS platforms.
              </Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.input}
                placeholder="Email"
                value={email}
                onChangeText={setEmail}
                autoCapitalize="none"
                keyboardType="email-address"
              />
              <TextInput
                style={styles.input}
                placeholder="Password"
                value={password}
                onChangeText={setPassword}
                secureTextEntry
              />
              {error ? <Text style={styles.errorText}>{error}</Text> : null}
              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.forgotPasswordButton} onPress={handleForgotPassword}>
                <Text style={styles.forgotPasswordButtonText}>Forgot Password?</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.signInButton} onPress={handleSignIn} disabled={loading}>
                {loading ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#fff" />
                ) : (
                  <Text style={styles.signInButtonText}>Sign In</Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.signInButtonWithIcon} onPress={signInWithGoogle}>
                <Image source={googleIcon} style={styles.icon} />
                <Text style={styles.signInButtonText}>Continue With Google</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </ImageBackground>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {

  return (
    <>
      <div>
        <a href="https://vitejs.dev" target="_blank">

        </a>

          <img src={reactLogo} className="logo react" alt="React logo" />
        </a>
      </div>

        </p>
      </div>

}

import React from 'react';
import { Page, Text, View, Document, StyleSheet } from '@react-pdf/renderer';
import Duty from './Duty';

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    page: {
      flexDirection: 'column',
      backgroundColor: '#E4E4E4',
      padding: 10
    },
    tableRow: {
      flexDirection: "row",
      borderBottomWidth: 1,
      borderBottomColor: '#bfbfbf',
      borderStyle: "solid",
      alignItems: 'center',
      height: 24
    },
    tableColHeader: {
      width: "25%",
      borderRightWidth: 1,
      borderRightColor: '#bfbfbf',
      borderStyle: "solid",
      padding: 5,
      fontWeight: 'bold'
    },
    tableCol: {
      width: "25%",
      borderRightWidth: 1,
      borderRightColor: '#bfbfbf',
      borderStyle: "solid",
      padding: 5
    },
    tableCell: {
      fontSize: 10
    }
  });
  

interface ReportDocumentProps {
    duties: Duty[];
  }

  const ReportDocument: React.FC<ReportDocumentProps> = ({ duties }) => {
    return (
      <Document>
        <Page size="A4" style={styles.page}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'column', marginBottom: 20 }}>
            <View style={styles.tableRow}>
              <Text style={styles.tableColHeader}>Duty ID</Text>
              <Text style={styles.tableColHeader}>Start</Text>
              <Text style={styles.tableColHeader}>End</Text>
              <Text style={styles.tableColHeader}>Trips</Text>
            </View>
            {duties.map((duty, index) => (
              <View key={index} style={styles.tableRow}>
                <Text style={styles.tableCol}>{duty.id}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.tableCol}>{duty.startTime}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.tableCol}>{duty.endTime}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.tableCol}>
                  {duty.trips.map((trip, index) => `Trip ID: ${trip.id}`).join(", ")}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
          <Text style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>Summary: Total Duties - {duties.length}</Text>
        </Page>
      </Document>
    );
  }
  
  export default ReportDocument;
